    <!-- ===== MUSINGS ===== -->
    <div class="lore-section" data-loresection="musings">
      <article class="musing">
        <h3>"On the Bow"</h3>
        <div class="meta">A musing · originally a fan letter, now widely cited</div>
        <p>The Wolves, after they kill, sometimes bow. This is not a rule. The Wolves did not always do this. The Wolves started doing this in Season 6, after the Final at Brewer's Reach, after the Healer.</p>
        <p>I do not think the Wolves are sorry. I think the Wolves think the dead are sacred. I think the Wolves are pack animals, and to a pack animal a fallen opponent is still part of the pattern of the pack. The bow is not an apology. The bow is an acknowledgment.</p>
        <p>This is why the bow has been adopted by no other team. The bow is wolf physics. It does not translate.</p>
        <p>I do not, however, think the Sharks should make fun of it.</p>
        <div class="signoff">— anonymous fan letter, reprinted in <em>The Long Bow</em>, vol. 2</div>
      </article>

      <article class="musing">
        <h3>"Why a Healer Wanders"</h3>
        <div class="meta">A musing · author: a former Healer who now writes for the Beaver paper</div>
        <p>People ask me, often, why I sometimes drifted across the field during my matches. They saw it on the broadcast. They thought it was poor coaching. It was not.</p>
        <p>A Healer's job is, technically, to heal the lowest-HP teammate. A Healer's actual job is to keep the team alive.</p>
        <p>Sometimes the lowest-HP teammate is a Tank, and the Tank is fine, because the Tank has plate. Sometimes the lowest-HP teammate is a Speedster, and the Speedster will not stay still long enough to be healed. Sometimes the lowest-HP teammate is in a position where, if I follow them, I will die. So I do not follow them. I follow someone else, or I drift toward the center to keep my options open. The drift is not laziness. The drift is patience.</p>
        <p>What I am trying to say is that a Healer who looks aimless is, in fact, doing the most thinking on the field. Snipers, please remember this. (You will not.)</p>
        <div class="signoff">— guest column, <em>The Lobster Reach Daily</em></div>
      </article>

      <article class="musing">
        <h3>"On the Long Marketing Group"</h3>
        <div class="meta">A musing · this is what the Beavers actually think · do not print</div>
        <p>The League is funded, in part, by an organization called the Long Marketing Group. They own the broadcast rights. Their slogan is "If it's not on LMG, it didn't happen."</p>
        <p>This is why some matches do not happen.</p>
        <p>I am writing this in confidence and you are not supposed to repeat it. The LMG decides which matches get coverage. The matches that do not get coverage are not in the standings. They are off the books. They are <em>training matches,</em> per the rule book. They are also, sometimes, real matches that the LMG simply chose not to broadcast, for reasons that are, you may imagine, not always good.</p>
        <p>The Sharks have lost three matches that were, on paper, training matches. The Beavers have won two of those. The standings do not reflect this. The standings reflect what the LMG broadcast.</p>
        <p>I am not saying we should burn down the LMG offices. I am saying that, if the LMG offices were to burn down accidentally, I would not personally call the fire engine on duty.</p>
        <div class="signoff">— anonymous, BVR coaching staff, "do not print"</div>
      </article>

      <article class="musing">
        <h3>"Concerning the Float Gas Tasters"</h3>
        <div class="meta">A musing · by someone who claims they were one</div>
        <p>I have been a Float Gas taster for the Boise Brawling Badgers for nine seasons. I am not a Badger. I will not say what I am. The Foundry pays me in cured salmon, twice a month, dropped at my back door in a box that does not have a return address.</p>
        <p>I taste each batch of Float Gas before it ships. I have tasted a lot of Float Gas. Float Gas tastes like cold pennies and a quiet song you used to know. Sometimes the song changes. When the song changes, I do not approve the batch. The Foundry then redoes the batch.</p>
        <p>I do not know what the song means. I do know that, in League Year 6, the song stopped, and the Foundry shut down, and the song came back, and the Foundry resumed. I do not have a theory about why. The Foundry has a theory. The Foundry has not shared it with me.</p>
        <p>I am writing this because someone should know. If I die in the next year, please tell my dog. The salmon is for the dog. The dog has a name. The Foundry knows it.</p>
        <div class="signoff">— anonymous, salmon delivery #2117</div>
      </article>

      <article class="musing">
        <h3>"On Permadeath"</h3>
        <div class="meta">A musing · by Coach Brask Ironclaw II, in the year of his son's death</div>
        <p>I lost my son this season. Brask the Third walked onto the field at Phoenix in Round 4 and did not walk off. He had three balloons. He used the third one finishing what he started. The League's report calls it "engagement complete." His mother and I call it Tuesday.</p>
        <p>I do not have a moral to share. People keep asking me what I have learned. I have learned nothing useful. I have learned that the League keeps every knight's helmet in a glass case in the Hall, and Brask's helmet is on a shelf next to his grandfather's, and the helmets <em>look the same</em>. I have learned that the Float Foundation will pay you for your kid in monthly installments and the installments are, in some technical sense, fair. I have learned that the Foxes have already drafted Brask's replacement.</p>
        <p>I have learned that the universe does not care that the Ironclaw line ended last Tuesday. The universe has a lot to do.</p>
        <p>I have not learned to forgive the Wolves. The Wolves did not kill him. The Wolves killed him three matches earlier in his career and he came back. I am going to spend the rest of my career not forgiving them anyway. The Wolves have offered to bow. They can keep their bow.</p>
        <div class="signoff">— Brask Ironclaw II, FOX coaching staff, retirement letter</div>
      </article>

      <article class="musing">
        <h3>On Lobster</h3>
        <div class="meta">A musing · concession-row gossip · Boston, Brewer's Reach, ongoing</div>
        <p>The lobster vendor has been at Brewer's Reach since before the inaugural match. This is documented. There are photographs of the field on Match Day One, and the lobster booth is in them, already weathered, with a sign already missing the same letter (an "S," from "STEAMED"). The booth has not moved since. The vendor has not changed. She is older than every Beaver currently on roster. She is older than the Commissioner. She is, by some accounts, older than the founding paperwork.</p>
        <p>Foundation Day — the annual celebration of the inaugural match — is the only date the lobster vendor leaves Brewer's Reach. She catered the first Foundation Day in League Year 2 (apparently as a personal gesture; the LMG had not yet incorporated and had not yet asked her). She has catered every Foundation Day since. She returns the morning after, at the same hour, in the same coat, with no luggage. Where she stays during her single night away is a topic of recurring concession-row debate. The leading theories are: (a) Helena, but no one in Helena has ever seen her; (b) underwater with the Otters, but the Otters have never confirmed; (c) she does not stay anywhere — she walks the night out and arrives the morning after on foot. Theory (c) does not explain how she gets to League Foundation Hall in the first place.</p>
        <p>The Float Foundation pays its larger settlements in lobster. This is widely understood as a quirk of the Foundation's accounting and dismissed as eccentric. The Foundation receives its lobster from one supplier. The supplier is the Brewer's Reach vendor. The Foundation has no other lobster source. The Foundation's bookkeepers have, on three occasions, attempted to acquire a backup supplier; on three occasions the backup supplier has been found to have closed shop the next morning, with no explanation, and the lobsters in the secondary fridge spoiled overnight. The Foundation has stopped trying.</p>
        <p>The vendor's prices have not changed since LY 1. Three coppers for a lobster roll. Five for a steamed pair. Lobster bisque is "available, ask," which has meant, in practice, that you have to be somebody to get the bisque. Bristlebeard the First got the bisque. Bristlebeard the Second did not. The Commissioner gets the bisque. The Beavers' current Tank does not. Crash Wartooth, on his last visit to Boston, was offered the bisque without asking; he declined politely. He was killed three matches later. Beavers fans who track these things consider the bisque a death omen. The Wolves do not.</p>
        <p>Some fans believe the lobster vendor is immortal. Some fans are correct.</p>
        <div class="signoff">— Concession-Row Gazette, columns 1 through 47, condensed</div>
      </article>
    </div>
    <div class="lore-section" data-loresection="timeline">
      <article class="history">
        <h3>The League Timeline</h3>
        <div class="meta">Maintained by the Float Foundation Archive · league years</div>
        <p>The events below use League Years (LY), counted from the founding of the Boston Beavers and the inaugural match at Brewer's Reach. The Collapse is in the unstated past; the League's current season is LY 8.</p>
        <div class="lore-timeline">
          <ol>
            <li><span class="yr">PRE-LEAGUE</span><div class="ev">The Collapse. North American power grids, banks, and telecommunications fail simultaneously. Cities reorganize around whatever they have. Boston rallies around its engineers. Tampa rallies around its salvage crews. Reno rallies around its slot machines. The Hedgehogs of Helena are already running backyard tournaments and pay no attention.</div></li>
            <li><span class="yr">LY 1</span><div class="ev">Boston Brisk Beavers founded. First match held at Brewer's Reach Pitch. The lobster vendor is already there. Old Bristlebeard the First dies defending the final round of the inaugural championship (S1 R5), taking three Beavers with him. His Riot Shield enters circulation as the league's first scavenged legacy item.</div></li>
            <li><span class="yr">LY 2</span><div class="ev">Wichita Wild Wolves founded. The Pack arrives. The Bow ritual is observed informally.</div></li>
            <li><span class="yr">LY 3</span><div class="ev">Tampa Tornado Sharks and Boise Brawling Badgers founded. The Foundry begins distilling Float Gas. Tomik Splashfang the Bold (SHK Brawl, S3 R2) fumbles a frag grenade in Tampa's third match ever and takes two Beavers with him. The Sharks retire his number for the season.</div></li>
            <li><span class="yr">LY 4</span><div class="ev">Phoenix Furious Foxes and Kansas Kicking Kits founded. Commissioner Whiskerstone takes office. The Long Marketing Group is incorporated, securing exclusive broadcast rights from a single salvaged Tampa tower. Brask Ironclaw III (FOX Striker, S4 R4) dies at Phoenix; his father retires from coaching.</div></li>
            <li><span class="yr">LY 5</span><div class="ev">Seattle Sleepless Owls and Fresno Frantic Frogs founded. Old Bristlebeard the Second (BVR Tank, S5 R1) walks into Otter fire on the opening kickoff of the season. The Beavers do not field a Bristlebeard for two years.</div></li>
            <li><span class="yr">LY 5</span><div class="ev">Vela Hootington (OWL Speed, S5 R7) dies holding a 22-kill streak in OWL's inaugural season. The next two knights to attempt to break the streak die in the same round trying. Mira Pawington (KIT Speed, S5 R6) dies completing her career as the Scavenger Queen, with her opponent's Power Cell still in her hand.</div></li>
            <li><span class="yr">LY 6</span><div class="ev">Portland Punching Pandas and Olympia Outlaw Otters founded. The Boise Float Gas Foundry stops production for forty-one days. No explanation given. The Badgers play with reserve gas and finish 2-12. An unsigned letter arrives at the League Office; nobody acknowledges it.</div></li>
            <li><span class="yr">LY 6</span><div class="ev">Bristlebeard's Riot Shield is lost on the field at the season's final, en route to the vault. The Shield disappears in transit. The Reno Heist begins. The Beavers are still angry.</div></li>
            <li><span class="yr">LY 7</span><div class="ev">Reno Rascal Raccoons founded. Crash Wartooth (WLF Brawl, S6 R3 — recently posthumous) is stunned three times in twelve seconds and dies; the Crash Rule is enacted league-wide. The Stadium Wars begin: Reno tries to buy Brewer's Reach, Tampa tries to buy the Foundry, Helena tells everyone "no" forty-eight times. Whiskerstone settles the dispute by sitting on every disputed deed for one consecutive month.</div></li>
            <li><span class="yr">LY 7</span><div class="ev">The Marshmallow Incident. Felix Marshmallow (PND Tank, S7 R5) is killed by friendly grenade fire. The Marshmallow Protocol is enacted; pre-season grenade safety drills are now mandatory.</div></li>
            <li><span class="yr">LY 8</span><div class="ev">Helena Hidden Hedgehogs founded — the newest formal League team, though the Hedgehogs as a faction have been fighting in informal tournaments for generations. Hester Quillstrike (HED Sniper) makes her debut at the Helena Glacier Rink and, in HED's third match (S8 R3), sets the 412-pixel longest-shot record. She dies in the same match to a coordinated four-Otter rush. Her Railgun Rifle is scavenged.</div></li>
            <li><span class="yr">LY 8</span><div class="ev">Sundip Hootington Jr. (OWL Sniper, S8 R8) carrying Hester's Rifle, ties the 412-pixel record at the Reno Open Court — matching it exactly without breaking it. He climbs the Power Core to celebrate. The Power Core overloads. He does not survive. The Frog Speedster he had just shot also does not survive. The League patches the overload that off-season. Hester's Rifle falls at the base of the Core and is scavenged in cleanup.</div></li>
            <li><span class="yr">CURRENT</span><div class="ev">The League continues. The Riot Shield is somewhere. The Foundry hums. The Wolves bow. The Healers wander. The Snipers shoot the Healers. The Float Foundation pays out in lobster, in installments. The lobster vendor is still there. The 412-pixel record is still co-held, posthumously, by two snipers who never met.</div></li>
          </ol>
        </div>
      </article>
    </div>

    <!-- ===== GLOSSARY ===== -->
    <div class="lore-section" data-loresection="glossary">
      <article class="history lore-glossary">
        <h3>Glossary of Terms</h3>
        <div class="meta">Reference · maintained by the League Office Editorial Board</div>
        <dl>
          <dt>The 412-Pixel Mark</dt>
          <dd>The League's longest single-shot kill on record, measured in arena broadcast-feed coordinates. Set by Hester Quillstrike (HED Sniper) at the Helena Glacier Rink in HED's inaugural season (LY 8 R3). Tied — exactly, never broken — by Sundip Hootington Jr. (OWL Sniper) at the Reno Open Court later that same year. The mark is now jointly held, posthumously, by both shooters.</dd>

          <dt>The Balloon Contract</dt>
          <dd>The standard agreement signed by every Knight before joining a roster. Specifies that a Knight is allotted three balloons per match, popped one-per-knockout. The third pop ends the Knight's match (and, after enough cumulative pops across their career, ends the Knight). Allegedly proposed by Splinter Tooth in League Year 1.</dd>

          <dt>The Bow</dt>
          <dd>A Wolf-pack ritual, first observed at Brewer's Reach in Season 6. After a fallen opponent goes down, the Wolves on the field briefly form a circle around the body and the captain bows. Not adopted by any other team. The Wolves themselves consider it a form of acknowledgment, not apology.</dd>

          <dt>The Collapse</dt>
          <dd>The unspecified North American crisis that preceded the League's founding. Power grids, banking, and telecommunications failed simultaneously over a single night. The League does not require any Knight to have an opinion on the Collapse to play.</dd>

          <dt>Concession Row</dt>
          <dd>The strip of food vendors along the eastern boundary of Brewer's Reach Pitch, Boston. Notable for the lobster vendor who has been there since before the inaugural match (LY 1) and has not, for any reason, ever moved her booth. Her prices have not changed since the League's founding. She is the Float Foundation's sole lobster supplier.</dd>

          <dt>The Crash Rule</dt>
          <dd>A Wolves' Brawler-only equipment rule, named for Crash Wartooth, requiring all Wolves Brawlers to wear heavy plate. Adopted in Season 7 after Wartooth was stunned three times in twelve seconds.</dd>

          <dt>Felix Drills</dt>
          <dd>The unofficial in-locker-room name for the Marshmallow Protocol's grenade-safety training. Mandatory league-wide since Season 7.</dd>

          <dt>The Float Foundation</dt>
          <dd>A neutral non-profit jointly funded by the League and the LMG. Operates the Hall of Fallen Heroes, pays out benefits to surviving family of permadeath Knights (often in lobster, by family request), and runs the Foundation Hall venue for Foundation Day events.</dd>

          <dt>Float Gas</dt>
          <dd>The proprietary lifting gas that fills League-spec balloons. Distilled at the Boise Float Gas Foundry. Each team receives a different formula: Boise gets the heaviest batch (with regulators); Olympia gets the lightest; Phoenix gets the desert formula (high-altitude expansion); etc. Composition is patented and undisclosed. The Foundry shut down for 41 days in League Year 6 with no explanation.</dd>

          <dt>The Float Gas Tasters</dt>
          <dd>A small group of (allegedly non-Badger) individuals retained by the Foundry to taste each batch of Float Gas before it ships. Paid in cured salmon. Their job is to detect "the song" — an undefined sensory quality of approved Float Gas.</dd>

          <dt>Foundation Day</dt>
          <dd>Annual league holiday celebrating the inaugural match at Brewer's Reach. The Holiday Festive (League Foundation Hall) biome is in rotation during this event.</dd>

          <dt>The Founding Years</dt>
          <dd>League Years 1 through 8 — the period during which the League's twelve formal teams were progressively admitted (BVR in LY 1, HED in LY 8). Most of the heroes in the Hall of Fallen died during these years, and most of the league's institutional rules (the Crash Rule, the Marshmallow Protocol, the Stadium Wars settlement, the Riot Shield's seven-carrier saga) were established in this period.</dd>

          <dt>Hallowtide</dt>
          <dd>League-wide week-long autumnal event. The Halloween (Reno Hallowtide Hollow) biome is the host venue. Wichita Wolves famously play their best games during Hallowtide.</dd>

          <dt>The Hedgehog Tradition</dt>
          <dd>The informal Helena combat circuit that predates HED's formal League admission by several generations. Backyard ranges, neighborhood three-on-three brawls, and mountain-pass exhibition matches gave Hedgehog snipers in particular a sustained training environment outside League oversight. This is why Hester Quillstrike, a rookie in HED's inaugural season (LY 8), was already capable of setting the league's longest-shot record (the 412-Pixel Mark) in her debut year.</dd>

          <dt>The Long Marketing Group (LMG)</dt>
          <dd>Post-Collapse advertising consortium that owns the League's broadcast rights. Slogan: "If it's not on LMG, it didn't happen." Famously antagonistic with the Commissioner's office. Funds half of the Float Foundation in exchange for tax write-offs.</dd>

          <dt>The Marshmallow Protocol</dt>
          <dd>League-wide grenade safety regulation enacted after the Marshmallow Incident in Season 7 of LY 7. Mandates pre-season grenade-safety drills for all teams. Officially named for Felix Marshmallow; informally called "Felix Drills."</dd>

          <dt>The Pawington Tradition</dt>
          <dd>A Kits-only scavenging ritual where the scavenger kneels by the fallen and asks permission before taking gear. Originated with Mira Pawington. Bench officials have on at least one occasion claimed to hear the corpse <em>respond</em>; that bench official was sent home.</dd>

          <dt>Permadeath</dt>
          <dd>The condition triggered when a Knight uses up the last of their three lives in a match. Permadeath is final. The Knight's gear drops on the field for survivors to scavenge; their helmet is placed in the Hall of Fallen Heroes; their family receives Float Foundation benefits.</dd>

          <dt>The Reno Heist</dt>
          <dd>The presumed (officially: alleged, possibly imagined) theft of Bristlebeard's Riot Shield in transit to the vault after Season 6's final. The Shield has since been seen on a Raccoon roster, then stolen back, then re-stolen, then re-stolen. Its current location is unknown and is being looked for. The lookers are tired.</dd>

          <dt>Reserve Gas</dt>
          <dd>The lighter, alternative Float Gas formula the Boise Foundry distributed during the LY 6 forty-one-day shutdown. Reserve gas burns cleaner but lifts less; matches played on it have a slightly different feel that veterans claim to detect. The Badgers, who normally receive the heaviest formula and rely on it, finished the LY 6 season 2-12. Reserve Gas is not currently in production. Its formula is not, officially, known to anyone outside the Foundry's senior chemist; unofficially it is widely suspected of being known to whoever wrote the unsigned Foundry letter that arrived in LY 6.</dd>

          <dt>Scavenging</dt>
          <dd>Picking up gear from a fallen Knight on the field. Scavenged gear retains its previous owner's name in the lineage chain (e.g., "Vela's Plate Cuirass"). Items with high scavenge counts become legendary; the most-scavenged item in League history is Bristlebeard's Riot Shield (7 carriers across 6 seasons).</dd>

          <dt>The Stadium Wars</dt>
          <dd>The Season 7 inter-city dispute when Reno, Tampa, and Fresno tried to buy each other's home stadiums. Resolved by Commissioner Whiskerstone, who sat on every contested deed for one consecutive month. Established the permanent League rule that stadiums cannot be sold; this is why neutral venues exist.</dd>

          <dt>The Steward's Pocket</dt>
          <dd>Locker-room shorthand for the bizarre incident in the LY 6 vault rotation, when a steward repeatedly arrived at the vault with a sealed bag containing one fewer item than the bag had been sealed with. The phrase "steward's pocket" now describes any object that goes missing without rational explanation.</dd>

          <dt>Tactical Erasure</dt>
          <dd>Sniper jargon, originating with the Tampa Sharks scouts' shared journal: a kill on a Healer that determines the outcome of an engagement before the engagement has even begun. Many Snipers find the term distasteful but most consider it accurate.</dd>

          <dt>Tactical Self-Sacrifice</dt>
          <dd>The League's official designation for the act of intentionally absorbing fatal damage to deny the kill or its effect to the opposing team. First codified after Old Bristlebeard the First ate a grenade in the inaugural championship final to deny it to the Wolves. Counts as a normal kill in the stats but is decorated separately in the Hall.</dd>
        </dl>
      </article>
    </div>

  </div>
</div>

<!-- ============== ABOUT TAB ============== -->
<div class="tab-content" data-tab="about">
  <div class="about-page">
    <h2>ℹ️ About Float Knights</h2>
    <div class="sub">An auto-playing mascot tournament sim. Cute, cuddly <b>dodgeball but with guns</b> — that's the closest description. Cute animals in medieval armor wielding futuristic weapons. HP = balloons. Death is permanent (eventually).</div>

    <div class="credits">
      <div class="block">
        <h3>How it works</h3>
        <p><b>12 teams</b> in the league. Each season runs a single-elimination bracket — 4 teams by default, configurable up to 12 in Settings. Each match is best-of-5 rounds. Each round is a <b>7-on-7</b> elimination fight (recently expanded — Healers and Grenadiers joined the original five archetypes).</p>
        <p>A round averages around 40-45 seconds. A full match runs 3-5 minutes. Bets are placed between rounds.</p>
        <p>Knights have <b>3 lives per match</b>. When KO'd in a round, they respawn the next round — <b>unless they've used all 3 lives</b>, in which case they die permanently and their gear drops on the field for survivors to scavenge. Scavenged gear keeps its previous owner's name and accumulates a lineage chain across the league's history.</p>
      </div>

      <div class="block">
        <h3>The seven archetypes</h3>
        <ul style="line-height:1.7;">
          <li><b>🛡️ Tank</b> — heavy plate, slow movement, soaks hits. Doesn't dodge.</li>
          <li><b>⚔️ Striker</b> — balanced offense, the tournament's workhorse class.</li>
          <li><b>💨 Speedster</b> — squishy, fast, the best dodger on the field.</li>
          <li><b>👊 Brawler</b> — close-quarters specialist with crit potential.</li>
          <li><b>🎯 Sniper</b> — long-range, glass cannon. Snipers preferentially target healers.</li>
          <li><b>✚ Healer</b> — support unit. Heals the lowest-HP teammate over time. Has a minimum range (won't melee), so it physically follows whoever it's healing. May wander across the battlefield when switching heal targets.</li>
          <li><b>💣 Grenadier</b> — throws ONE grenade at the start of an engagement, dealing AoE damage. Then falls back to weak melee — bring 'em early.</li>
        </ul>
      </div>

      <div class="block">
        <h3>What the AI actually does</h3>
        <ul style="line-height:1.7;">
          <li>Shows mercy to low-HP enemies; flees on low HP toward health pickups</li>
          <li>Hunts down attackers (mercy state); swarms toward visible enemies</li>
          <li>Picks up and uses items, scavenges fallen gear if it's an upgrade</li>
          <li>Navigates around terrain blocks (corner-stuck strafe to round walls)</li>
          <li><b>Dodges incoming projectiles</b> — Speedsters dodge often, Snipers rarely, Tanks not at all. Per-bot cooldown so dodges punctuate, not dominate. Low-HP bots in melee can dodge BACKWARDS to disengage from scrums.</li>
          <li>Random crits and misses; weapon inaccuracy creates natural variance</li>
          <li>Snipers don't camp the corner anymore; they kite to ~50% of weapon range</li>
          <li>Pushes the center for the Power Core when a teammate is closest</li>
          <li>Knockback on every hit, scaled by damage and reduced by armor</li>
        </ul>
      </div>

      <div class="block">
        <h3>Match details</h3>
        <ul style="line-height:1.7;">
          <li>Player names are <b>massively</b> randomized (~4.1 billion combinations) — you won't see a duplicate twice in a lifetime</li>
          <li>Projectiles have physics: velocity, range, swept collision, pierce-prevention through walls</li>
          <li>Bots, terrain, and projectiles all collide; dropped items have hitboxes</li>
          <li>Field obstacle placement is randomized per match (Power Core gets a guaranteed clear zone in the center)</li>
          <li>Match outcomes are <b>seeded and procedurally generated</b> — same seed reproduces the same match exactly, every time</li>
          <li>Sim speed is adjustable (bottom-right of the arena)</li>
          <li>The bottom <b>stock-ticker</b> rolls live scores, top streaks, lost-loot callouts, and recent feed events</li>
        </ul>
      </div>

      <div class="block">
        <h3>The economy</h3>
        <p>You earn "bits" by placing bets on matches you watch. Bet markets: Match Winner, Round Winner, Last Knight Standing, First Kill, Total Casualties (over/under), Match Length (over/under).</p>
        <p>Spend bits at the Store — three rarity tiers (Common, Rare, Legendary) of buffs and debuffs. Sandbag the opponent's whole team, curse them with -10 HP, or splash an Elixir on your roster for the legendary treatment.</p>
      </div>

      <div class="block">
        <h3>Inspirations</h3>
        <ul>
          <li><i>Blaseball</i> — permanent mortality + emergent narrative</li>
          <li><i>Mario Kart 64 Battle Mode</i> — balloons as HP</li>
          <li><i>Worms</i> — death cries on KO + gear pickup chaos</li>
          <li><i>Salem / UO</i> — items named after their dead carriers</li>
        </ul>
      </div>

      <div class="block">
        <h3>Roadmap (still cooking)</h3>
        <ul>
          <li><b>AI sportscaster commentary</b> — live narration of the action via the ai-text-plugin</li>
          <li><b>Replay viewer</b> — re-watch any past match (made possible by the deterministic seeded sim)</li>
          <li><b>Cross-user shared league</b> — every visitor sees the same match in progress at the same time, via uploadPlugin checkpoints</li>
          <li><b>Bet-strategy analytics</b> — your win rate by market type, longest losing streak, favorite team to bet on</li>
          <li><b>Custom team colors / emoji</b></li>
        </ul>
      </div>

      <div class="block">
        <h3>Credits</h3>

<!-- ============================================================
     SIMULATION — Part A
     Constants, name generator, position archetypes, league state
     ============================================================ -->
<script>
'use strict';

/* ============================================================
   RNG + MATH HELPERS
   All randomness used by the simulation routes through SIM_RNG, a
   seeded mulberry32 PRNG. Two clients running with the same seed
   produce byte-identical match outcomes — the foundation for shared
   live matches (see ROADMAP.md Tier 1).

   `Math.random()` is preserved for non-simulation purposes (UI
   nicety, visual jitter that doesn't affect outcome). The helpers
   below (R, RI, pick, pickN, shuffle) ALWAYS use the seeded RNG.
   ============================================================ */

// mulberry32 — fast, good distribution, period 2^32
function mulberry32(seed) {
  let s = seed >>> 0;
  return function() {
    s = (s + 0x6D2B79F5) >>> 0;
    let t = s;
    t = Math.imul(t ^ (t >>> 15), t | 1);
    t ^= t + Math.imul(t ^ (t >>> 7), t | 61);
    return ((t ^ (t >>> 14)) >>> 0) / 4294967296;
  };
}

// Hash a string to a uint32 (FNV-1a). Used for stable match seeds.
function hashStr(s) {
  let h = 2166136261 >>> 0;
  for (let i = 0; i < s.length; i++) {
    h ^= s.charCodeAt(i);
    h = Math.imul(h, 16777619);
  }
  return h >>> 0;
}

// Deterministic seed for a given (season, slot) pair. The genesis seed is
// stable per league (set once on initLeague + persisted), so two clients
// loading the same saved state derive the same match seed.
function computeMatchSeed(season, slot) {
  const genesis = (typeof LEAGUE !== "undefined" && LEAGUE.genesisSeed) || 0xF107C001;
  return hashStr(`${genesis}:s${season}:${slot}`);
}

// Global RNG state for the simulation. Seeded at match start (and
// inherited from saved league state for determinism across reloads).
const SIM_RNG = {
  seed: 0,
  next: () => Math.random(), // overwritten on first reseed
  reseed(seed) {
    this.seed = seed >>> 0;
    this.next = mulberry32(this.seed);
  },
};
// Separate RNG for visual-only biome/scatter generation. Reseeded at
// match start with a DIFFERENT transformation of the match seed so its
// stream is independent of the combat RNG. This guarantees that adding
// or removing a biome decoration call in the future never affects
// combat outcomes — visuals and gameplay use independent streams.
const BIOME_RNG = {
  seed: 0,
  next: () => Math.random(),
  reseed(seed) {
    // XOR with a separate constant so this stream diverges from SIM_RNG
    // even when both are seeded from the same match seed.
    this.seed = (seed ^ 0x9E3779B9) >>> 0;
    this.next = mulberry32(this.seed);
  },
};
// Initial seed — replaced as soon as the first match starts. Using a
// fixed integer here means a fresh league always boots identically
// until startMatch() reseeds with a per-match seed.
SIM_RNG.reseed(0xF107C001);
BIOME_RNG.reseed(0xF107C001);

function R(min, max) { return SIM_RNG.next() * (max - min) + min; }
function RI(min, max) { return Math.floor(R(min, max + 1)); }
function pick(arr) { return arr[Math.floor(SIM_RNG.next() * arr.length)]; }
function pickN(arr, n) {
  const c = arr.slice();
  const out = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < n && c.length; i++) {
    out.push(c.splice(Math.floor(SIM_RNG.next() * c.length), 1)[0]);
  }
  return out;
}
function shuffle(arr) {
  const a = arr.slice();
  for (let i = a.length - 1; i > 0; i--) {
    const j = Math.floor(SIM_RNG.next() * (i + 1));
    [a[i], a[j]] = [a[j], a[i]];
  }
  return a;
}
function clamp(v, lo, hi) { return Math.max(lo, Math.min(hi, v)); }
function distance(a, b) {
  const dx = a.x - b.x, dy = a.y - b.y;
  return Math.sqrt(dx*dx + dy*dy);
}

/* ============================================================
   NAME GENERATOR
   Embedded from user's "simple random name generator" (4-syllable
   fantasy-style: Capital + lowercase + middle syllable + ending).
   Produces names like "Qarchlut", "Bearson", "Acackadain".

   To swap in a different generator:
     - Replace the four arrays below, OR
     - Replace genFirstName() to call your source.
   ============================================================ */
const NG_W1 = ["Q","A","B","C","D","E","F","G","H","I","J","K","L","M","N","O","P","R","S","T","U","V","W","X","Y","Z"];
const NG_W2 = ["a","c","e","f","h","l","m","n","o","r","s","t","u","x","y"];
const NG_W3 = ["rch","a","ack","ad","agg","ah","ai","al","an","ana","ar","ara","as","b","c","ch","ck","d","dwyn","e","ea","ear","ee","eira","ek","el","end","er","ex","ey","f","g","gn","h","i","iar","ig","il","ill","im","imma","in","in","inu","io","ion","ip","ir","iri","irm","irsa","is","ish","isil","ito","iz","izu","j","ka","ko","l","la","lar","lbor","le","le","les","ll","lm","ln","ls","m","me","mm","mo","n","na","nd","ne","nk","nt","nt","ntin","o","ock","og","om","on","oo","ory","our","p","ph","r","ra","rc","re","ren","ri","rn","ro","rr","rra","rv","s","se","sh","sh","sl","so","t","ta","te","tz","u","ull","us","us","ve","x","y","ynn","yo","zer"];
const NG_W4 = ["lut","aam","adain","adon","ain","aira","ais","ako","alary","ald","aliam","aline","ama","amari","ami","an","anan","ander","ankem","ano","aot","ar","ari","aroch","aron","ary","as","asha","asia","ay","azan","bbert","brone","cinbe","ck","cko","dai","dain","dalis","ddery","den","dian","dor","eah","eal","eam","ean","edain","ee","een","eif","enain","enar","enard","endon","eous","era","erm","eron","ery","ery","ethel","eve","even","eybol","fer","fir","firem","frey","gal","garom","gawa","gen","ggy","gil","gin","gon","gout","ham","haru","haun","helle","heo","hera","hina","hon","ht","iador","iam","ian","ias","iba","ice","igan","iko","ill","ilus","imor","ine","inus","ion","ira","is","ita","ka","katt","ker","ket","key","ku","la","lain","lash","ld","le","lem","ley","liaf","ll","lliden","loki","ma","main","mar","mara","maros","mer","mi","mie","mon","mor","morn","ndus","ndy","nell","ner","net","nny","nor","ntz","nuary","oconi","odenn","odor","off","old","om","omi","omoru","onton","onus","oole","oot","oot","oram","orm","orn","ory","ouhm","oum","ouos","ous","pher","pike","ra","rah","ran","rd","rea","ree","rem","ren","ri","rio","rlie","rom","ron","ros","rot","rry","run","rynn","shima","shin","son","sose","ta","tac","tain","tall","tar","ter","terian","thain","thor","thorne","tide","to","tone","tor","uce","udain","uib","ull","us","use","use","va","vid","von","wran","wrath","xander","xia","xieam","xoust","yle","zell","zuo"];

function genFirstName() {
  return pick(NG_W1) + pick(NG_W2) + pick(NG_W3) + pick(NG_W4);
}

/* Mascot surnames — give the fantasy first name a sports-flavored last name */
const SURNAMES = [
  "Whiskers","Pawington","Snouter","Furr","Nibbles","Talons","Scales","Thunderclaw",
  "Finsworth","Tailwhip","Brushfoot","Glimmerfang","Hootington","Bubblesnap","Dustfeather",
  "Bristle","Mossback","Quillbeard","Velvetfoot","Pebble","Ironclaw","Dapplecoat",
  "Wartooth","Fizzlepaw","Marshmallow","Pricklesnoot","Chompers","Whisper",
  "Hopstride","Dribblepaw","Snufflenose","Crashfoot"
];
const SUFFIXES = ["", "", "", "", "", "II", "III", "Jr.", "the Bold", "the Ferocious", "the Cute"];

function genFullName() {
  let parts = [genFirstName(), pick(SURNAMES)];
  const sx = pick(SUFFIXES);
  if (sx) parts.push(sx);
  return parts.join(" ");
}

    slotType: "weapon", lostBy: "Sundip Hootington Jr.",
    lineage: ["Hester Quillstrike", "Sundip Hootington Jr.", "Vela Hootington", "Sundip Hootington Jr."],
    epitaph: "Three of the four carriers were Owls. The Hedgehogs swore vengeance. The vengeance is still pending." },
  { name: "Mira's Stealth Cloak (scavenged)", ico: "🧥", scavengedCount: 3, season: 6, slot: "sf1",
    slotType: "armor", lostBy: "Felix Marshmallow",
    lineage: ["Mira Pawington", "Crash Wartooth", "Felix Marshmallow"],
    epitaph: "A speed-built Cloak ended up on a Tank, who promptly tripped over a terrain block and lost it. Fans called the moment 'The Stumble.'" },
  { name: "Tomik's Stun Baton (scavenged)", ico: "🔱", scavengedCount: 3, season: 5, slot: "qf2",
    slotType: "weapon", lostBy: "Vela Hootington",
    lineage: ["Tomik Splashfang the Bold", "Brask Ironclaw III", "Vela Hootington"],
    epitaph: "Vela picked it up at 5% HP and was stunned to death by an Otter wielding a copy of the same baton 4 seconds later." },
  { name: "Brask's Brigandine (scavenged)", ico: "🎽", scavengedCount: 3, season: 7, slot: "final",
    slotType: "armor", lostBy: "Felix Marshmallow",
    lineage: ["Brask Ironclaw III", "Felix Marshmallow", "Crash Wartooth"],
    epitaph: "The only armor in league history known to have switched teams (Foxes → Pandas → Wolves) before being lost." },
];

/* ============================================================
   COMBAT CAPS & TUNABLES
   ============================================================ */
const CAPS = { hp: 240, dmg: 40, def: 70, spd: 4 };
const COMBAT = {
  baseCrit: 0.08,
  critMult: 1.7,        // crit damage multiplier (was 2.0; reduced to slow lethality)
  rngSpread: 0.5,       // ±50% damage roll
  hitRange: 18,         // pixels — melee range
  arenaW: 960,          // 16:9 canvas
  arenaH: 540,
  arenaSize: 960,       // back-compat alias (used by some helpers — equals arenaW)
  frameMs: 50,          // sim tick at 1×
  roundMaxFrames: 1500, // safety cap on a single ROUND (longer rounds need more)
  matchMaxFrames: 1500, // alias kept for back-compat
  spawnRadius: 380,     // distance from center where teams spawn (wider arena)
  // Armor formula: damage_taken = damage * (1 - def/(def + DEF_DENOM))
  // Lower DEF_DENOM = armor matters more. 35 is meaningfully tougher than 50.
  defDenom: 35,
  // Cooldown multiplier on all attacks (1.0 = unchanged, >1 = slower).
  cdMult: 1.20,
};

/* ============================================================
   MATCH STRUCTURE — best-of-N rounds + lives per match
   Each "match" in the bracket (semifinal, final) is best-of-5
   rounds. Each round is a 5v5 elimination fight. Knights have
   3 lives per match — a round-loss costs them one. Run out of
   lives and they die permanently, dropping their gear.
   ============================================================ */
const MATCH = {
  bestOf: 5,           // number of rounds total
  roundsToWin: 3,      // first-to-N (must be ceil(bestOf/2))
  startingLives: 3,    // each player gets this many lives per match
  interRoundDelay: 0,  // milliseconds (0 = pause for user input)
};

/* ============================================================
   EQUIPMENT — weapons, armor, items
   Each player carries a loadout {weapon, armor, item}. When they
   die, the loadout drops at their corpse and surviving players
   may scavenge it. Picked-up gear is renamed
     "<owner>'s <gear name> (scavenged)"
   and persists with that name (cascading if scavenged again).
   ============================================================ */

// Each weapon defines combat modifiers and reach. `range` 0-30 = melee
// instant-hit; >30 = projectile-based ranged. `projSpeed` = px/frame for
// the projectile. `inacc` = baseline aim cone in radians (more = wilder).
const WEAPONS = [
  { id: "shield",  ico: "🛡",  name: "Riot Shield",     defMod: +6, dmgMod: -2, cdMod:  0,    critMod:  0,    stun: 0,    range: 22,  projSpeed: 0,  inacc: 0,    flavorPos: "tank" },
  { id: "baton",   ico: "🔱", name: "Stun Baton",      defMod:  0, dmgMod:  0, cdMod:  0,    critMod:  0,    stun: 0.18, range: 22,  projSpeed: 0,  inacc: 0,    flavorPos: "striker" },
  { id: "laser",   ico: "🔫", name: "Laser Pistol",    defMod:  0, dmgMod: -1, cdMod: -0.30, critMod:  0,    stun: 0,    range: 230, projSpeed: 14, inacc: 0.05, flavorPos: "speed" },
  { id: "mace",    ico: "🔨", name: "Plasma Mace",     defMod:  0, dmgMod: +3, cdMod:  0,    critMod: +0.02, stun: 0,    range: 26,  projSpeed: 0,  inacc: 0,    flavorPos: "brawl" },
  { id: "railgun", ico: "📡", name: "Railgun Rifle",   defMod: -2, dmgMod: +6, cdMod: +0.30, critMod: +0.05, stun: 0,    range: 460, projSpeed: 22, inacc: 0.025, flavorPos: "sniper" },
  // Healer wand — DMG 0 in combat. Special-cased: when wielded by a healer
  // it triggers the heal-beam behavior (see tickHealer). Listed as a weapon
  // so scavenging/loadout systems work, but it does nothing offensive.
  { id: "wand",    ico: "✨", name: "Healing Wand",    defMod:  0, dmgMod: -10, cdMod:  0,    critMod:  0,    stun: 0,    range: 90,  projSpeed: 0,  inacc: 0,    flavorPos: "healer" },
  // Grenade launcher — fires one slow projectile that explodes on impact.
  // Long cooldown, heavy damage in an AoE radius.
  { id: "launcher", ico: "💣", name: "Grenade Launcher", defMod: -1, dmgMod: +4, cdMod: +0.80, critMod: -0.05, stun: 0,    range: 220, projSpeed: 6,  inacc: 0.05, flavorPos: "grenadier" },
];
const WEAPON_BY_ID = Object.fromEntries(WEAPONS.map(w => [w.id, w]));

const ARMORS = [
  { id: "plate",   ico: "🪖", name: "Plate Cuirass",   defMod: +10, spdMod:  0,    critMod:  0,    flavorPos: "tank" },
  { id: "brig",    ico: "🎽", name: "Brigandine",      defMod:  +5, spdMod:  0,    critMod:  0,    flavorPos: "striker" },
  { id: "leather", ico: "🧣", name: "Padded Leathers", defMod:  +2, spdMod: +0.3,  critMod:  0,    flavorPos: "speed" },
  { id: "chain",   ico: "⛓",  name: "Chainmail",       defMod:  +6, spdMod: -0.1,  critMod:  0,    flavorPos: "brawl" },
  { id: "cloak",   ico: "🧥", name: "Stealth Cloak",   defMod:  +3, spdMod: +0.1,  critMod: +0.05, flavorPos: "sniper" },
  { id: "robe",    ico: "🥻", name: "Medic Robe",      defMod:  +2, spdMod: +0.2,  critMod:  0,    flavorPos: "healer" },
  { id: "vest",    ico: "🦺", name: "Flak Vest",       defMod:  +4, spdMod:  0,    critMod:  0,    flavorPos: "grenadier" },
];
const ARMOR_BY_ID = Object.fromEntries(ARMORS.map(a => [a.id, a]));

// Items have a trigger: "start" (fires once at match begin),
// "passive" (active for whole match), "lowHP" (fires once when HP <30%).
const ITEMS = [
  { id: "stim",      ico: "🍶", name: "Healing Stim",    trigger: "lowHP",   desc: "Restores 25 HP at <30%" },
  { id: "booster",   ico: "💨", name: "Booster Pack",    trigger: "start",   desc: "+0.5 SPD for 12s" },
  { id: "visor",     ico: "🎯", name: "Targeting Visor", trigger: "passive", desc: "+5% crit chance" },
  { id: "grenade",   ico: "🧨", name: "Frag Grenade",    trigger: "lowHP",   desc: "Hits nearest enemy for 18 at <25%" },
  { id: "powercell", ico: "🔋", name: "Power Cell",      trigger: "start",   desc: "+25% DMG for 15s" },
];
const ITEM_BY_ID = Object.fromEntries(ITEMS.map(i => [i.id, i]));

// "Score" a piece of gear so the AI can compare its current piece to
// a scavengable upgrade. Higher = better for that slot.
// Crucially, this is now POSITIONAL — a Sniper values a Railgun more than
// a stronger-on-paper weapon they don't know how to use, so we don't get
// every survivor swapping to the absolute strongest piece.
function gearScore(slot, gear, ownerPosition) {
  if (!gear) return 0;
  if (slot === "weapon") {
    let s = gear.dmgMod * 2.5 + gear.defMod * 1.2 + (-gear.cdMod) * 6 + gear.critMod * 80 + gear.stun * 25;
    if (ownerPosition && gear.flavorPos === ownerPosition) s += 5;
    if (ownerPosition === "sniper" && gear.range > 200) s += 4;
    if (ownerPosition === "speed"  && gear.range > 100) s += 2;
    if ((ownerPosition === "tank" || ownerPosition === "brawl") && gear.range < 50) s += 3;
    return s;
  } else if (slot === "armor") {
    // Armor is now MORE valuable — defMod weighted 2.5x (was 1.5), spdMod 8x.
    // This is paired with the new defDenom=35 making each point of def
    // meaningfully reduce damage, so the AI's preference reflects actual impact.
    let s = gear.defMod * 2.5 + gear.spdMod * 8 + gear.critMod * 80;
    if (ownerPosition && gear.flavorPos === ownerPosition) s += 5;
    // Position-specific armor preferences
    if (ownerPosition === "tank" && gear.defMod >= 8) s += 4;   // tanks LOVE heavy armor
    if (ownerPosition === "speed" && gear.spdMod > 0) s += 4;   // speedsters love mobility
    if (ownerPosition === "sniper" && gear.critMod > 0) s += 3; // snipers love crit boost
    return s;
  } else if (slot === "item") {
    return 5;
  }
  return 0;
}

// Equip gear on a player and stamp the scavenged name if applicable.
// `gear` is a clone of a base WEAPON/ARMOR/ITEM definition with optional
// `displayName` and `originalOwner` fields when scavenged.
function gearDisplayName(gear) {
  if (!gear) return "";
  return gear.displayName || gear.name;
}

/* ============================================================
   PLAYER FACTORY (updated)
   Each new player spawns with a loadout matching their position by
   default — Tanks get Riot Shields & Plate Cuirass, Snipers get
   Railguns & Stealth Cloaks, etc. Items are randomized for variety.
   ============================================================ */
let _idCounter = 1;

function rollBaseStats() {
  return {
    hp:  RI(140, 200),  // was 70-120 — doubled for ~45s rounds
    dmg: RI(8, 22),
    def: RI(8, 30),     // was 5-25 — slightly higher floor
    spd: +(R(1.4, 2.6).toFixed(2)),
  };
}

function makePlayer(teamId, position) {
  const base = rollBaseStats();
  const bias = position.bias;
  const stats = {
    hp:  clamp(base.hp + bias.hp, 30, CAPS.hp),
    dmg: clamp(base.dmg + bias.dmg, 4, CAPS.dmg),
    def: clamp(base.def + bias.def, 0, CAPS.def),
    spd: clamp(+(base.spd + bias.spd).toFixed(2), 0.6, CAPS.spd),
    crit: clamp(COMBAT.baseCrit + bias.crit, 0.02, 0.30),
  };
  // Default loadout by position (with a fresh cloned object so scavenged
  // copies can be renamed without mutating the catalog).
  const defaultWeapon = WEAPONS.find(w => w.flavorPos === position.id) || WEAPONS[0];
  const defaultArmor  = ARMORS.find(a => a.flavorPos === position.id)  || ARMORS[0];
  const randomItem    = pick(ITEMS);
  return {
    id: _idCounter++,
    name: genFullName(),
    teamId,
    position: position.id,
    stats,
    // Loadout — start with default gear for the position
    weapon: { ...defaultWeapon },
    armor:  { ...defaultArmor },
    item:   { ...randomItem },
    // Career stats (persist across matches/seasons)
    career: { kills: 0, deaths: 0, matchesPlayed: 0, matchesWon: 0, seasonsPlayed: 0, scavenged: 0, longestStreak: 0, currentStreak: 0 },
    // Mortality
    deceased: false,
    deceasedSeason: null,
    deceasedBy: null,
    debutSeason: 1,
    // Match-time fields (reset per match)
    x: 0, y: 0, vx: 0, vy: 0,
    hp: stats.hp,
    alive: true,
    lives: MATCH.startingLives, // per-match lives (resets at match start)
    target: null,
    stunned: 0,        // frames remaining stunned (skip attacks)
    itemUsed: false,   // whether the consumable has fired this match
    boostMods: null,   // {dmg, spd, life} active boost from item
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   LEAGUE STATE — top-level game state
   ============================================================ */
const LEAGUE = {
  season: 1,
  bits: 0,
  // Roster: { fox: [5 players], bvr: [5 players], owl: [5 players], shk: [5 players] }
  rosters: {},
  // Permanent graveyard: array of deceased players (never reaped)
  graveyard: [],
  // Team records: { teamId: { wins, losses, championships } }
  records: {},
  // Bracket for current season:
  // { semi1: {teamA, teamB, winner|null, kills}, semi2: {...}, final: {...} }
  bracket: null,
  // Match in progress (live state during a fight)
  match: null,
  // Active match index in bracket order: "semi1" | "semi2" | "final" | null
  currentSlot: null,
  // History of past seasons: [{season, champion, runnerup, casualties}]
  history: [],
  // Active bets (cleared after each match resolves)
  bets: [],
  // Shop boosts pending: { teamId: { dmg, def, spd, ... } } — applied next match
  pendingBoosts: {},
};

function initLeague(opts) {
  opts = opts || {};
  // Genesis seed: stable across this league's lifetime. If supplied (e.g. for
  // tests / replay), use that. Otherwise generate a random one — the only
  // place real Math.random affects sim outcomes. Once set, persists in saves.
  LEAGUE.genesisSeed = opts.genesisSeed || ((Math.random() * 0xFFFFFFFF) >>> 0);
  // Reseed the sim RNG NOW so all setup randomness (rosters, names, etc.)
  // is derived from genesisSeed deterministically.
  SIM_RNG.reseed(LEAGUE.genesisSeed);

  for (const team of TEAMS) {
    LEAGUE.rosters[team.id] = POSITIONS.map(p => makePlayer(team.id, p));
    LEAGUE.records[team.id] = { wins: 0, losses: 0, draws: 0, championships: 0 };
  }
  LEAGUE.season = 1;
  LEAGUE.bits = 0;
  LEAGUE.graveyard = [];
  LEAGUE.history = [];
  LEAGUE.matchHistory = [];
  LEAGUE.lostItems = [];
  LEAGUE.bets = [];
  LEAGUE.pendingBoosts = {};
  LEAGUE.startedAt = Date.now();   // when this league was first booted (immutable)
  LEAGUE.lastTickAt = Date.now();  // wall-clock of most recent sim activity (updated each frame)
  buildBracket();
}

/* ============================================================
   BRACKET SCHEDULER — random seeding each season.
   Supports three formats (set via LEAGUE_CONFIG.bracketSize):
     4  → semi1, semi2, final
     8  → qf1..qf4, sf1, sf2, final
     12 → ql1..ql4 (qualifier round, 8 teams), then qf1..qf4, sf1, sf2, final
          (Top 4 ranked teams get byes; bottom 8 play qualifiers.)
   The bracket is stored as an ordered list of slots so advanceBracket()
   can step through them generically.
   ============================================================ */
function buildBracket() {
  const size = (LEAGUE_CONFIG && LEAGUE_CONFIG.bracketSize) || 4;
  const teams = shuffle(TEAMS.map(t => t.id));
  LEAGUE.bracket = { _order: [] };

  if (size === 4) {
    // Pick 4 random teams from the league
    const chosen = teams.slice(0, 4);
    LEAGUE.bracket.semi1 = { teamA: chosen[0], teamB: chosen[1], winner: null, casualties: [], depends: null };
    LEAGUE.bracket.semi2 = { teamA: chosen[2], teamB: chosen[3], winner: null, casualties: [], depends: null };
    LEAGUE.bracket.final = { teamA: null, teamB: null, winner: null, casualties: [], depends: ["semi1","semi2"] };
    LEAGUE.bracket._order = ["semi1", "semi2", "final"];
  } else if (size === 8) {
    const chosen = teams.slice(0, 8);
    for (let i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
      LEAGUE.bracket["qf" + (i+1)] = { teamA: chosen[i*2], teamB: chosen[i*2+1], winner: null, casualties: [], depends: null };
    }
    LEAGUE.bracket.sf1 = { teamA: null, teamB: null, winner: null, casualties: [], depends: ["qf1","qf2"] };
    LEAGUE.bracket.sf2 = { teamA: null, teamB: null, winner: null, casualties: [], depends: ["qf3","qf4"] };
    LEAGUE.bracket.final = { teamA: null, teamB: null, winner: null, casualties: [], depends: ["sf1","sf2"] };
    LEAGUE.bracket._order = ["qf1","qf2","qf3","qf4","sf1","sf2","final"];
  } else if (size === 12) {
    // Top 4 by championships (then by wins) get byes; bottom 8 play qualifiers
    const ranked = TEAMS.slice().sort((a, b) => {
      const ra = LEAGUE.records[a.id] || { championships: 0, wins: 0 };
      const rb = LEAGUE.records[b.id] || { championships: 0, wins: 0 };
      return (rb.championships - ra.championships) || (rb.wins - ra.wins);
    }).map(t => t.id);
    const byeTeams = ranked.slice(0, 4);
    const qualifierTeams = shuffle(ranked.slice(4));
    // 4 qualifier matches → 4 winners join the 4 byes for the 8-team ladder
    for (let i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
      LEAGUE.bracket["ql" + (i+1)] = { teamA: qualifierTeams[i*2], teamB: qualifierTeams[i*2+1], winner: null, casualties: [], depends: null, isQualifier: true };
    }
    // Quarterfinals: each bye-team paired with one qualifier winner
    LEAGUE.bracket.qf1 = { teamA: byeTeams[0], teamB: null, winner: null, casualties: [], depends: ["ql1"] };
    LEAGUE.bracket.qf2 = { teamA: byeTeams[1], teamB: null, winner: null, casualties: [], depends: ["ql2"] };
    LEAGUE.bracket.qf3 = { teamA: byeTeams[2], teamB: null, winner: null, casualties: [], depends: ["ql3"] };
    LEAGUE.bracket.qf4 = { teamA: byeTeams[3], teamB: null, winner: null, casualties: [], depends: ["ql4"] };
    LEAGUE.bracket.sf1 = { teamA: null, teamB: null, winner: null, casualties: [], depends: ["qf1","qf2"] };
    LEAGUE.bracket.sf2 = { teamA: null, teamB: null, winner: null, casualties: [], depends: ["qf3","qf4"] };
    LEAGUE.bracket.final = { teamA: null, teamB: null, winner: null, casualties: [], depends: ["sf1","sf2"] };
    LEAGUE.bracket._order = ["ql1","ql2","ql3","ql4","qf1","qf2","qf3","qf4","sf1","sf2","final"];
  }

  LEAGUE.currentSlot = LEAGUE.bracket._order[0];
}

function advanceBracket() {
  // Step through the bracket order, populating teams from dependencies.
  const order = LEAGUE.bracket._order || ["semi1", "semi2", "final"];
  const idx = order.indexOf(LEAGUE.currentSlot);
  if (idx === -1 || idx + 1 >= order.length) {
    LEAGUE.currentSlot = null;
    return;
  }
  LEAGUE.currentSlot = order[idx + 1];
  // Resolve dependencies: pull team IDs from earlier slots' winners
  const slot = LEAGUE.bracket[LEAGUE.currentSlot];
  if (slot.depends) {
    if (slot.teamA == null && slot.depends[0]) slot.teamA = LEAGUE.bracket[slot.depends[0]].winner;
    if (slot.teamB == null && slot.depends[1]) slot.teamB = LEAGUE.bracket[slot.depends[1]].winner;
    // 12-team qf slots have only one dependency (the qualifier winner fills slot B)
    else if (slot.teamB == null && slot.depends[0] && slot.teamA) {
      slot.teamB = LEAGUE.bracket[slot.depends[0]].winner;
    }
  }
}

function activeMatchTeams() {
  if (!LEAGUE.currentSlot) return null;
  const m = LEAGUE.bracket[LEAGUE.currentSlot];
  return { slot: LEAGUE.currentSlot, teamA: m.teamA, teamB: m.teamB, match: m };
}

/* ============================================================
   ROSTER MAINTENANCE — replacement of deceased between matches
   Mortality is permanent; rookies replace dead at the SAME position
   so each team always has one of each archetype.
   ============================================================ */
function reapDeceased() {
  // Move dead players from active rosters to graveyard, then call up rookies.
  const callups = [];
  for (const team of TEAMS) {
    const roster = LEAGUE.rosters[team.id];
    for (let i = 0; i < roster.length; i++) {
      const p = roster[i];
      if (p.deceased) {
        // Already dead — make sure they're in graveyard, then replace
        const pos = POS_BY_ID[p.position];
        const rookie = makePlayer(team.id, pos);
        rookie.debutSeason = LEAGUE.season;
        roster[i] = rookie;
        callups.push({ team: team.id, rookie, replaced: p });
      }
    }
  }
  return callups;
}

   Carried over from v2 — purchased between matches, applied once.
   ============================================================ */

  { id: "elixir",  ico: "🧉", nm: "Elixir of Endurance", cost: 150, rarity: "legendary", desc: "+50 HP and +10 DEF, whole team",
    apply: (boost) => { boost.hp = (boost.hp||0)+50; boost.def = (boost.def||0)+10; } },
  { id: "curse",   ico: "💀", nm: "Curse of the Loser", cost: 175, rarity: "legendary", desc: "Opponent: -10 HP and -5 DEF and -3 DMG",
    apply: (boost) => { boost.hp = (boost.hp||0)-10; boost.def = (boost.def||0)-5; boost.dmg = (boost.dmg||0)-3; }, target: "opp" },
];
const SHOP_BY_ID = Object.fromEntries(SHOP_ITEMS.map(s => [s.id, s]));

function applyBoosts(teamId, players) {
  // Apply pending boost (if any) to a team's players for this match.
  const boost = LEAGUE.pendingBoosts[teamId];
  if (!boost) return;
  for (const p of players) {
    if (boost.hp)   p.hp = Math.min(CAPS.hp, p.hp + boost.hp);
    if (boost.dmg)  p.stats = { ...p.stats, dmg:  clamp(p.stats.dmg + boost.dmg,  1, CAPS.dmg) };
    if (boost.def)  p.stats = { ...p.stats, def:  clamp(p.stats.def + boost.def,  0, CAPS.def) };
    if (boost.spd)  p.stats = { ...p.stats, spd:  clamp(+(p.stats.spd + boost.spd).toFixed(2), 0.6, CAPS.spd) };
    if (boost.crit) p.stats = { ...p.stats, crit: clamp(p.stats.crit + boost.crit, 0.02, 0.50) };
  }
  // Boosts consumed
  delete LEAGUE.pendingBoosts[teamId];
}

</script>

<!-- ============================================================
     SIMULATION — Part B
     Match init, team-aware AI, combat, match-end, bracket flow
     ============================================================ -->
<script>
'use strict';

/* ============================================================
   COMMENTARY FEED (in-memory, capped)
   ============================================================ */
const FEED = [];
const FEED_MAX = 60;

function feed(kind, msg) {
  const ts = LEAGUE.match
    ? `${String(Math.floor(LEAGUE.match.frame / 20)).padStart(2,"0")}:${String(LEAGUE.match.frame % 20 * 5).padStart(2,"0")}`
    : "--:--";
  FEED.unshift({ kind, msg, ts });
  if (FEED.length > FEED_MAX) FEED.length = FEED_MAX;
  if (typeof renderFeed === "function") renderFeed();
}

/* ============================================================
   SPORTSCASTER COMMENTARY — separate channel from the kill feed.
   Pulls from large tonally-categorized pools and pushes lines as
   they happen. Optional AI enhancement via ai-text-plugin (hook
   at the bottom): if the plugin is present and responds, it can
   replace the static line with a fresher take. Static pool
   ALWAYS fires first so there's no latency hole.

   Channels: "play-by-play" (raw event description) and "color"
   (analyst-style observation about the meta of the moment).
   ============================================================ */
const COMMENTARY = [];      // most-recent first
const COMMENTARY_MAX = 40;

function commentate(kind, line, opts) {
  if (!line) return;
  opts = opts || {};
  const ts = LEAGUE.match
    ? `${String(Math.floor(LEAGUE.match.frame / 20)).padStart(2,"0")}:${String(LEAGUE.match.frame % 20 * 5).padStart(2,"0")}`
    : "--:--";
  const entry = {
    kind,                                 // "play"|"color"|"chant"|"opener"
    line,
    ts,
    flavor: opts.flavor || null,          // "hype"|"deadpan"|"sad"|"chaotic"
    isAi: !!opts.isAi,
    id: COMMENTARY_MAX_ID++,
  };
  COMMENTARY.unshift(entry);
  if (COMMENTARY.length > COMMENTARY_MAX) COMMENTARY.length = COMMENTARY_MAX;
  if (typeof renderCommentary === "function") renderCommentary();
  // Async AI enhancement — does NOT block the sim. If the plugin returns
  // a different/better line, it updates the entry in place.
  if (opts.aiContext && typeof requestAiCommentaryEnhancement === "function") {
    requestAiCommentaryEnhancement(entry, opts.aiContext);
  }
  return entry;
}
let COMMENTARY_MAX_ID = 1;

function ccPick(pool, vars) {
  let s = pick(pool);
  for (const k of Object.keys(vars || {})) {
    s = s.replaceAll("{" + k + "}", vars[k]);
  }
  return s;
}

  // Real implementation lives in the events layer where it can reach

}

/* ============================================================

const DEATH_CRIES_GENERIC = [

function deathCryFor(defender, attacker, ctx) {
  // ctx: { isCrit, isPermadeath, wasOnStreak, wasStunned, isSelfDestruct }
  ctx = ctx || {};
  let pool;
  if (ctx.isSelfDestruct) pool = DEATH_CRIES_SELFDESTRUCT;
  else if (ctx.isPermadeath) pool = DEATH_CRIES_LASTLIFE;
  else if (ctx.wasOnStreak) pool = DEATH_CRIES_STREAKBROKEN;
  else if (ctx.wasStunned) pool = DEATH_CRIES_STUNNED;
  else if (ctx.isCrit) pool = DEATH_CRIES_CRIT;
  else pool = DEATH_CRIES_GENERIC;
  return pick(pool);
}

   ============================================================ */

function taunt(phrase, p, m) {
  if (!phrase || !p || !m) return;
  m.floats.push({
    x: p.x, y: p.y - 22,
    text: phrase,
    life: 50,
    color: "#f7c948",
  });
  feed("kill", `📣 ${p.name}: "${phrase}"`);
}
function phrasePick(pool, vars) {
  let s = pick(pool);
  for (const k of Object.keys(vars)) {
    s = s.replaceAll("{" + k + "}", vars[k]);
  }
  return s;
}

/* ============================================================
   MATCH INITIALIZATION (best-of-N rounds)
   - startMatch() sets up match-level state, resets lives, then
     immediately starts round 1.
   - startRound() fields all eligible players (lives > 0, !deceased),
     positions them, fires "start" items on round 1, sets phase = "round".
   ============================================================ */
function startMatch() {
  if (!LEAGUE.currentSlot) return;
  const b = LEAGUE.bracket[LEAGUE.currentSlot];
  if (!b.teamA || !b.teamB) return;

  // SAFETY NET — ensure both teams have full living rosters BEFORE we filter.
  const callups = reapDeceased();
  for (const c of callups) {
    feed("spawn", `📣 Emergency call-up: ${c.rookie.name} replaces ${c.replaced.name} (${TEAM_BY_ID[c.team].name}).`);
  }

  // DETERMINISTIC SEED — same season + slot ALWAYS produces the same match.
  // This is the foundation for shared live matches: two clients running with
  // the same league state will see identical kill orders, dodges, drops, and
  // outcomes. Each match's seed is a hash of (season, slot, season-genesis).
  const matchSeed = computeMatchSeed(LEAGUE.season, LEAGUE.currentSlot);
  SIM_RNG.reseed(matchSeed);
  BIOME_RNG.reseed(matchSeed);

  const teamA = LEAGUE.rosters[b.teamA].filter(p => !p.deceased);
  const teamB = LEAGUE.rosters[b.teamB].filter(p => !p.deceased);

  // Reset match-level state on every player going into this match
  for (const p of [...teamA, ...teamB]) {
    p.lives = MATCH.startingLives;
    p.itemUsed = false;
    p.boostMods = null;
    p.balloonsPopped = 0;
  }

  // Apply pending shop boosts (consumed once per match)
  applyBoosts(b.teamA, teamA);
  applyBoosts(b.teamB, teamB);

  LEAGUE.match = {
    slot: LEAGUE.currentSlot,
    teamAId: b.teamA, teamBId: b.teamB,
    teamAplayers: teamA, teamBplayers: teamB,
    bestOf: MATCH.bestOf,
    roundsToWin: MATCH.roundsToWin,
    roundsWonA: 0, roundsWonB: 0,
    rounds: [],
    currentRoundIndex: 0,
    phase: "preRound",
    frame: 0,
    roundFrame: 0,
    killsByTeam: { [b.teamA]: 0, [b.teamB]: 0 },
    firstKillTeam: null,
    blood: [],
    corpses: [],
    floats: [],
    drops: [],
    projectiles: [],
    terrain: generateTerrain(),  // randomized obstacles for this match
    biome: pickBiomeForMatch(),  // visual variety per match
    scatter: [],                 // populated below
    clouds: generateCloudShadows(),
    ambient: [],                 // populated below
    winnerId: null,
  };
  // Scatter + ambient have to be generated AFTER biome is set
  LEAGUE.match.scatter = generateBiomeScatter(LEAGUE.match.biome);
  LEAGUE.match.ambient = generateAmbientParticles(LEAGUE.match.biome);

  feed("match", phrasePick(PHRASE_MATCH_START, {
    a: TEAM_BY_ID[b.teamA].emoji + " " + TEAM_BY_ID[b.teamA].short,
    b: TEAM_BY_ID[b.teamB].emoji + " " + TEAM_BY_ID[b.teamB].short,
  }));

  // === Sportscaster opener + pre-match chants ===
  const tA = TEAM_BY_ID[b.teamA], tB = TEAM_BY_ID[b.teamB];
  commentate("opener", ccPick(COMM_MATCH_OPEN, {
    a: tA.emoji + " " + tA.short,
    b: tB.emoji + " " + tB.short,
  }), { flavor: "hype", aiContext: { type: "matchOpen", teamA: tA, teamB: tB } });

  // Each team shouts a chant — pulled from per-team chants array
  if (tA.chants && tA.chants.length) {
    commentate("chant", `${tA.emoji} ${tA.short}: "${pick(tA.chants)}"`, { flavor: "hype" });
  }
  if (tB.chants && tB.chants.length) {
    commentate("chant", `${tB.emoji} ${tB.short}: "${pick(tB.chants)}"`, { flavor: "hype" });
  }

  // Begin round 1 immediately
  startRound();
}

function startRound() {
  const m = LEAGUE.match;
  if (!m) return;
  m.currentRoundIndex++;
  m.phase = "round";
  m.roundFrame = 0;
  m.projectiles = m.projectiles || []; // ensure projectile array exists

  // Eligible players: not deceased AND have lives remaining
  const eligibleA = m.teamAplayers.filter(p => !p.deceased && p.lives > 0);
  const eligibleB = m.teamBplayers.filter(p => !p.deceased && p.lives > 0);

  if (!eligibleA.length || !eligibleB.length) {
    finalizeRound(/*forfeit*/ true);
    return;
  }

  layoutTeam(eligibleA, "left");
  layoutTeam(eligibleB, "right");

  // Spawn neutral items on the field. Round 1 gets the full set (3 Health
  // Packs + 2 Armor + 3 random); later rounds refresh if the field is
  // sparse (re-stock so survivors don't fight over scraps).
  if (m.currentRoundIndex === 1) {
    spawnNeutralItems(m, 8);
  } else {
    if (m.drops.length < 4) spawnNeutralItems(m, 4);
  }

  if (m.currentRoundIndex === 1) {
    for (const p of [...eligibleA, ...eligibleB]) {
      if (p.item && p.item.trigger === "start" && !p.itemUsed) {
        applyItemTrigger(p);
      }
    }
  }

  feed("match", `🔔 Round ${m.currentRoundIndex} of ${m.bestOf} — fight!`);
}

/* ============================================================
   POWER CORE — center-of-arena objective.
   Spawns ~10sec into the round and pulses with golden light.
   Walking into it grants your TEAM a damage buff for 8 sec.
   After capture, respawns 30 sec later. Gives AI a strong
   reason to push the center.
   ============================================================ */
function tickPowerCore(m) {
  if (!m.powerCore) {
    m.powerCore = { state: "spawning", spawnAtFrame: 200, x: COMBAT.arenaW / 2, y: COMBAT.arenaH / 2 };
  }
  const pc = m.powerCore;
  const frame = m.roundFrame;

  if (pc.state === "spawning" && frame >= pc.spawnAtFrame) {
    pc.state = "active";
    pc.spawnedFrame = frame;
    feed("item", `⚡ POWER CORE has spawned at the center of the arena!`);
  }

  if (pc.state === "active") {
    // Check if any alive knight is touching it
    const all = m.teamAplayers.concat(m.teamBplayers);
    for (const p of all) {
      if (!p.alive) continue;
      const dx = p.x - pc.x, dy = p.y - pc.y;
      if (dx*dx + dy*dy < 14*14) {
        // Captured!
        const team = TEAM_BY_ID[p.teamId];
        feed("item", `⚡ ${p.name} captures the POWER CORE — ${team.short} gets +25% DMG!`);
        m.floats.push({ x: pc.x, y: pc.y - 16, text: "CAPTURED", life: 40, color: team.color });
        // Apply team buff
        const teammates = (p.teamId === m.teamAId ? m.teamAplayers : m.teamBplayers);
        for (const t of teammates) {
          if (!t.alive) continue;
          if (!t.boostMods) t.boostMods = {};
          t.boostMods.dmgMult = 1.25;
          t.boostMods.life = 160; // 8 sec at 20fps
        }
        if (typeof window !== "undefined" && window.toast) {
          window.toast(`⚡ <b>${team.short}</b> captures POWER CORE — +25% damage for 8s`, "info", { ico: "⚡", ttl: 5000 });
        }
        // Sportscaster
        if (typeof commentate === "function") {
          commentate("play", ccPick(COMM_CORE_CAPTURE, { tm: team.emoji + " " + team.short }), { flavor: "hype" });
        }
        pc.state = "respawn";
        pc.respawnAtFrame = frame + 600; // 30 sec
        pc.lastCapturedTeam = p.teamId;
        pc.lastCapturedFrame = frame;
        break;
      }
    }
  } else if (pc.state === "respawn" && frame >= pc.respawnAtFrame) {
    pc.state = "active";
    pc.spawnedFrame = frame;
    feed("item", `⚡ POWER CORE respawns at the center.`);
  }
}

/* Spawn neutral items at match start. Mix:
   - Health Packs (instant +30 HP on pickup, NOT a gear slot)
   - Armor pieces (random armor — instant equip if upgrade)
   - General loot (random weapon/armor/item)
   This gives the AI reasons to scout the map besides chasing each other. */
function spawnNeutralItems(m, n) {
  const margin = 60;
  const safeSpawn = () => {
    let x, y, attempts = 0;
    do {
      x = R(margin, COMBAT.arenaW - margin);
      y = R(margin, COMBAT.arenaH - margin);
      attempts++;
    } while (m.terrain && collidesTerrain(x, y, m.terrain) && attempts < 12);
    return { x, y };
  };

  // Always spawn 4 Health Packs (was 3) — bigger HP pool needs more healing
  const healthCount = Math.min(n, 4);
  for (let i = 0; i < healthCount; i++) {
    const { x, y } = safeSpawn();
    m.drops.push({
      x, y,
      slot: "health",
      gear: { id: "healthpack", ico: "❤", name: "Health Pack", heal: 60 },
      fromName: null, neutral: true,
    });
  }

  // Always spawn 3 Armor pieces (was 2) — armor is now strategically vital
  for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
    const { x, y } = safeSpawn();
    m.drops.push({
      x, y,
      slot: "armor",
      gear: { ...pick(ARMORS) },
      fromName: null, neutral: true,
    });
  }

  // Fill the rest with general loot (random slot)
  const rest = Math.max(0, n - healthCount - 3);
  for (let i = 0; i < rest; i++) {
    const { x, y } = safeSpawn();
    const slot = pick(["weapon", "armor", "item"]);
    const catalog = slot === "weapon" ? WEAPONS : slot === "armor" ? ARMORS : ITEMS;
    m.drops.push({
      x, y, slot,
      gear: { ...pick(catalog) },
      fromName: null, neutral: true,
    });
  }
}

/* Periodic mid-round resupply: every 25 sec of round time, spawn one
   fresh Health Pack at a safe position. Keeps long matches sustainable. */
function maybeResupplyHealth(m) {
  if (!m.lastResupplyFrame) m.lastResupplyFrame = 0;
  if (m.roundFrame - m.lastResupplyFrame < 500) return; // 25s at 50ms ticks
  // Don't over-saturate the field
  const healthOnField = m.drops.filter(d => d.slot === "health").length;
  if (healthOnField >= 3) return;
  m.lastResupplyFrame = m.roundFrame;
  const margin = 60;
  let x, y, attempts = 0;
  do {
    x = R(margin, COMBAT.arenaW - margin);
    y = R(margin, COMBAT.arenaH - margin);
    attempts++;
  } while (m.terrain && collidesTerrain(x, y, m.terrain) && attempts < 12);
  m.drops.push({
    x, y, slot: "health",
    gear: { id: "healthpack", ico: "❤", name: "Health Pack", heal: 60 },
    fromName: null, neutral: true,
  });
  feed("item", `❤ Resupply: a Health Pack drops onto the field.`);
}

/* ============================================================
   TERRAIN GENERATION
   3-7 square obstacle blocks clustered near the center vertical
   line of the arena. Each block is randomly nudged left/right so
   no two matches feel identical. Blocks are ~36×36px (roughly 2×
   a player's footprint) and AI must path around them.
   ============================================================ */
function generateTerrain() {
  const blocks = [];
  const count = RI(3, 7);
  const SIZE = 36;
  const cx = COMBAT.arenaW / 2;
  const cy = COMBAT.arenaH / 2;
  const usable = COMBAT.arenaH - 80;
  // Power Core spawns at exact arena center with a ~14px radius. Reserve
  // a 70px clear zone around the center so terrain can never overlap it.
  // If a placed block would land in this exclusion zone, push it outward
  // along the y-axis (away from center) until it's clear.
  const CORE_KEEPOUT = 70;
  for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
    const yFrac = (i + 1) / (count + 1);
    let y = Math.round(40 + usable * yFrac - SIZE / 2);
    let x = Math.round(cx - SIZE / 2 + R(-90, 90));
    // Distance from block CENTER to arena center
    const blockCx = x + SIZE / 2, blockCy = y + SIZE / 2;
    const dx = blockCx - cx, dy = blockCy - cy;
    const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy);
    if (d < CORE_KEEPOUT + SIZE / 2) {
      // Push the block along its current vector from center until it clears.
      // If it's exactly on top of center (rare), pick a default down-direction.
      const ux = d > 0.5 ? dx / d : 0;
      const uy = d > 0.5 ? dy / d : 1;
      const need = (CORE_KEEPOUT + SIZE / 2) - d + 4; // 4px breathing room
      x = Math.round(x + ux * need);
      y = Math.round(y + uy * need);
      // Clamp inside arena bounds
      x = clamp(x, 8, COMBAT.arenaW - SIZE - 8);
      y = clamp(y, 8, COMBAT.arenaH - SIZE - 8);
    }
    blocks.push({ x, y, size: SIZE });
  }
  return blocks;
}

/* ============================================================
   BIOMES — visual variation across the play field.

   Each match is assigned a biome deterministically (via SIM_RNG so
   the same match seed always produces the same look). Biomes provide:
   - base + secondary floor colors for the gradient
   - mowing-stripe hue (subtle striped pattern, like a sports pitch)
   - scatter palette (decorative tufts/pebbles/petals scattered on floor)
   - accent palette (cracks, lines, wear marks)
   - sky tint (affects vignette mood)
   - ambient particle type ("petal", "ember", "snow", null)

   Biomes are drawn UNDER players, terrain, and projectiles. They are
   drawn EVERY frame (decorations are stored in match state, not
   re-randomized) — this is cheap because there are typically <200
   decoration sprites.
   ============================================================ */

const BIOME_BY_ID = Object.fromEntries(BIOMES.map(b => [b.id, b]));

/* Pick a biome for a match. Uses BIOME_RNG (independent stream) so
   biome variety doesn't shift combat outcomes. */
function pickBiomeForMatch() {
  return BIOMES[Math.floor(BIOME_RNG.next() * BIOMES.length)];
}

/* Generate decorative scatter for a match: an array of `{type, x, y, c1, c2,
   r, rot}` records. The coordinates avoid the spawn lanes (where knights
   stand at round start) and the center circle (where the Power Core lives).
   Generated once per match with BIOME_RNG (independent stream) so the
   layout is stable BUT doesn't affect combat outcomes. */
function generateBiomeScatter(biome) {
  if (!biome) return [];
  const out = [];
  const W = COMBAT.arenaW, H = COMBAT.arenaH;
  const cx = W / 2, cy = H / 2;
  const SPAWN_KEEPOUT = 80;
  const CORE_KEEPOUT = 75;
  for (const [typeKey, def] of Object.entries(biome.scatter || {})) {
    for (let i = 0; i < def.count; i++) {
      // Rejection sampling — keep clear zones spawn-free
      let x, y, tries = 0;
      do {
        x = Math.round(BIOME_RNG.next() * (W - 16) + 8);
        y = Math.round(BIOME_RNG.next() * (H - 16) + 8);
        tries++;
        if (tries > 8) break;
        const dxC = x - cx, dyC = y - cy;
        const distCenter = Math.hypot(dxC, dyC);
        if (distCenter < CORE_KEEPOUT) continue;
        // Avoid spawn columns (left + right edges)
        if (x < SPAWN_KEEPOUT || x > W - SPAWN_KEEPOUT) {
          // Allow some on the edges (only a fraction)
          if (BIOME_RNG.next() > 0.3) continue;
        }
        break;
      } while (true);
      out.push({
        type: typeKey,
        x, y,
        c1: def.color1, c2: def.color2,
        r: 1 + Math.floor(BIOME_RNG.next() * 3),
        rot: BIOME_RNG.next() * Math.PI * 2,
      });
    }
  }
  return out;
}

/* Generate cloud shadows for a match. 2-3 large soft rectangles that drift
   diagonally across the field. Uses BIOME_RNG for the same isolation reason. */
function generateCloudShadows() {
  const clouds = [];
  const count = 2 + Math.floor(BIOME_RNG.next() * 2);
  for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
    clouds.push({
      x: BIOME_RNG.next() * COMBAT.arenaW * 1.5 - COMBAT.arenaW * 0.25,
      y: BIOME_RNG.next() * COMBAT.arenaH * 1.2 - COMBAT.arenaH * 0.1,
      w: 280 + BIOME_RNG.next() * 220,
      h: 140 + BIOME_RNG.next() * 120,
      vx: 0.18 + BIOME_RNG.next() * 0.18,
      vy: -0.04 + BIOME_RNG.next() * 0.08,
      alpha: 0.07 + BIOME_RNG.next() * 0.05,
    });
  }
  return clouds;
}

/* Tick cloud shadows — drift them and wrap when they exit. Called from
   tickMatch each frame. */
function tickCloudShadows(m) {
  if (!m.clouds) return;
  for (const c of m.clouds) {
    c.x += c.vx;
    c.y += c.vy;
    // Wrap when fully out the right side
    if (c.x > COMBAT.arenaW + 100) c.x = -c.w - 100;
    if (c.y > COMBAT.arenaH + 100) c.y = -c.h - 100;
    if (c.y < -c.h - 100) c.y = COMBAT.arenaH + 100;
  }
}

/* ============================================================
   AMBIENT PARTICLES — biome-driven atmospheric effect.

   Snow falls in the Frozen Rink, embers rise in Volcanic Lot,
   petals drift in Cherry Blossom Garden, heat shimmer wavers in
   the Desert Pit. Stadium Pitch has no ambient (clear day).

   Particles are tracked in `m.ambient[]` with {x, y, vx, vy, life,
   r, type}. They are recycled when life expires or they exit the
   arena bounds. Cap at ~80 particles total to keep the cost
   bounded. Particles use BIOME_RNG since they're cosmetic-only.
   ============================================================ */
const AMBIENT_CAP = 80;

function generateAmbientParticles(biome) {
  if (!biome || !biome.ambient) return [];
  const particles = [];
  const initialCount = biome.ambient === "shimmer" ? 30 : 60;
  for (let i = 0; i < initialCount; i++) {
    particles.push(_spawnAmbientParticle(biome.ambient, true));
  }
  return particles;
}

/* Spawn a single particle. If `existing` is true, it spawns anywhere on the
   arena (so the field starts already populated). Otherwise it spawns at the
   recycle origin (top for snow/petal, bottom for ember). */
function _spawnAmbientParticle(kind, existing) {
  const W = COMBAT.arenaW, H = COMBAT.arenaH;
  if (kind === "snow") {
    return {
      type: "snow",
      x: BIOME_RNG.next() * W,
      y: existing ? BIOME_RNG.next() * H : -10,
      vx: -0.15 + BIOME_RNG.next() * 0.30,    // slow horizontal drift
      vy: 0.45 + BIOME_RNG.next() * 0.50,     // gentle fall
      r: 0.8 + BIOME_RNG.next() * 1.4,
      life: 9999,                              // recycled by bounds, not life
      phase: BIOME_RNG.next() * Math.PI * 2,  // for sway
    };
  }
  if (kind === "ember") {
    return {
      type: "ember",
      x: BIOME_RNG.next() * W,
      y: existing ? BIOME_RNG.next() * H : H + 10,
      vx: -0.25 + BIOME_RNG.next() * 0.50,
      vy: -0.40 - BIOME_RNG.next() * 0.45,    // float up
      r: 1.0 + BIOME_RNG.next() * 1.5,
      life: 80 + Math.floor(BIOME_RNG.next() * 60),
      maxLife: 0,  // set below
      phase: BIOME_RNG.next() * Math.PI * 2,
    };
  }
  if (kind === "petal") {
    return {
      type: "petal",
      x: BIOME_RNG.next() * W,
      y: existing ? BIOME_RNG.next() * H : -10,
      vx: 0.20 + BIOME_RNG.next() * 0.45,     // drift right with breeze
      vy: 0.30 + BIOME_RNG.next() * 0.45,
      r: 1.5 + BIOME_RNG.next() * 1.5,
      life: 9999,
