    if (!all.length) return null;
    all.sort((a,b) => (b.lives - a.lives) || (b.career.kills - a.career.kills));
    return all[0];
  })();

  const remaining = [];
  for (const bet of LEAGUE.bets) {
    if (bet.slot !== m.slot) {
      remaining.push(bet);
      continue;
    }
    if (bet.kind === "round") {
      LEAGUE.bits += bet.amount;
      feed("item", `↩️ Unsettled round bet refunded: ${bet.amount} bits.`);
      if (typeof window !== "undefined" && window.toast)
        window.toast(`Round bet refunded: <b>+${bet.amount}</b> bits (match ended early)`, "warn");
      continue;
    }
    let win = false;
    let label = "";
    if (bet.kind === "team") {
      label = TEAM_BY_ID[bet.target]?.short || "?";
      if (bet.target === winnerTeamId) win = true;
    }
    if (bet.kind === "player") {
      const p = Object.values(LEAGUE.rosters).flat().find(x => x.id === bet.target);
      label = p ? p.name : "?";
      if (lastStanding && bet.target === lastStanding.id) win = true;
    }
    if (bet.kind === "firstkill") {
      label = (TEAM_BY_ID[bet.target]?.short || "?") + " first kill";
      if (m.firstKillTeam && bet.target === m.firstKillTeam) win = true;
    }
    if (bet.kind === "totalcas") {
      // Casualty count = unique deceased knights this match
      const matchDeaths = LEAGUE.graveyard.filter(g => g.season === LEAGUE.season && g.slot === m.slot).length;
      const line = 5.5;
      label = bet.target === "over" ? `over ${line} deaths` : `under ${line} deaths`;
      if ((bet.target === "over" && matchDeaths > line) || (bet.target === "under" && matchDeaths < line)) win = true;
    }
    if (bet.kind === "rounds") {
      const totalRounds = m.rounds.length;
      const line = 3.5;
      label = bet.target === "over" ? `over ${line} rounds` : `under ${line} rounds`;
      if ((bet.target === "over" && totalRounds > line) || (bet.target === "under" && totalRounds < line)) win = true;
    }
    if (win) {
      const payout = Math.round(bet.amount * bet.payoutMult);
      LEAGUE.bits += payout;
      feed("item", `💸 Bet won! +${payout} bits (${bet.kind})`);
      if (typeof window !== "undefined" && window.toast)
        window.toast(`Match bet WON: <b>+${payout}</b> bits on ${label}`, "win");
    } else {
      feed("item", `💔 Bet lost: ${bet.amount} bits on ${bet.kind}.`);
      if (typeof window !== "undefined" && window.toast)
        window.toast(`Match bet lost: <b>-${bet.amount}</b> bits on ${label}`, "lose");
    }
  }
  LEAGUE.bets = remaining;

  // Always award some bits for watching
  const watchReward = 5 + RI(0, 5);
  LEAGUE.bits += watchReward;
  feed("item", `🪙 +${watchReward} bits (broadcast tip)`);
  if (typeof saveLocal === "function") saveLocal();
}

/* ============================================================
   SHOP
   ============================================================ */
function buyItem(itemId, teamId) {
  const item = SHOP_BY_ID[itemId];
  if (!item) return false;
  if (LEAGUE.bits < item.cost) return false;
  if (LEAGUE.match && LEAGUE.match.phase === "round") return false; // can't buy during a live round

  // Saboteur targets the OPPONENT in the next active match
  let appliedToTeam = teamId;
  if (item.target === "opp") {
    if (!LEAGUE.currentSlot) return false;
    const m = LEAGUE.bracket[LEAGUE.currentSlot];
    appliedToTeam = (teamId === m.teamA) ? m.teamB : m.teamA;
  }

  const boost = LEAGUE.pendingBoosts[appliedToTeam] || (LEAGUE.pendingBoosts[appliedToTeam] = {});
  item.apply(boost);
  LEAGUE.bits -= item.cost;
  feed("item", `🛒 Purchased ${item.nm} for ${TEAM_BY_ID[appliedToTeam].name}.`);
  if (typeof saveLocal === "function") saveLocal();
  return true;
}

</script>

<!-- ============================================================
     UI — Part A
     Canvas pixel-art renderer + DOM render functions
     ============================================================ -->
<script>
'use strict';

/* ============================================================
   ELEMENT REFS
   ============================================================ */
const $ = (sel) => document.querySelector(sel);
const $$ = (sel) => Array.from(document.querySelectorAll(sel));
const arenaCanvas = $("#arena");

// Camera shake — set by triggerShake(magnitude); decays each frame.
// Drawn as a CSS transform on the canvas wrapper so it doesn't fight
// the canvas's own coordinate system.
let _shakeAmp = 0;
function triggerShake(amp) {
  // Settings toggle — skip if user has disabled camera shake
  if (typeof LEAGUE_CONFIG !== "undefined" && LEAGUE_CONFIG.disableShake) return;
  _shakeAmp = Math.max(_shakeAmp, amp);
}
function applyShake() {
  if (typeof document === "undefined") return;
  const wrap = document.querySelector(".arena-wrap");
  if (!wrap) return;
  if (_shakeAmp <= 0.05) {
    wrap.style.transform = "";
    _shakeAmp = 0;
    return;
  }
  const t = (typeof performance !== "undefined" ? performance.now() : Date.now());
  // Smooth high-frequency jitter
  const dx = Math.sin(t * 0.07) * _shakeAmp;
  const dy = Math.cos(t * 0.09) * _shakeAmp * 0.7;
  wrap.style.transform = `translate(${dx.toFixed(2)}px, ${dy.toFixed(2)}px)`;
  _shakeAmp *= 0.78; // exponential decay
}
/* `ctx` is the global drawing context — bound to the main arena canvas
   by default. It's declared with `let` (not `const`) so the biome-preview
   helper can temporarily redirect drawing to a thumbnail canvas, run the
   existing scatter+court rendering routines (which all reference this
   global), and then restore it. The redirect happens synchronously
   within drawBiomePreview() so no other render call can interleave. */
let ctx = arenaCanvas.getContext("2d");
ctx.imageSmoothingEnabled = false;

/* Decorative scenery — fixed positions for 16:9 arena (960×540) */
const SCENERY = [
  { type: "pillar", x: 200, y: 130 },
  { type: "pillar", x: 760, y: 130 },
  { type: "pillar", x: 200, y: 410 },
  { type: "pillar", x: 760, y: 410 },
  { type: "banner", x: 480, y:  60 },
  { type: "banner", x: 480, y: 480 },
  { type: "rubble", x: 340, y: 280 },
  { type: "rubble", x: 620, y: 280 },
  { type: "rubble", x: 480, y: 200 },
  { type: "rubble", x: 480, y: 340 },
];

// Convert "#RRGGBB" to "rgba(r,g,b,a)" for compositing
function _hexA(hex, a) {
  const c = (hex || "#888").replace("#", "");
  const r = parseInt(c.substring(0,2), 16);
  const g = parseInt(c.substring(2,4), 16);
  const b = parseInt(c.substring(4,6), 16);
  return `rgba(${r},${g},${b},${a})`;
}

/* Blend two "#RRGGBB" colors at the given ratio (0=a, 1=b). Used to
   produce a smooth midpoint for the floor gradient. */
function _blend(hexA, hexB, t) {
  const ca = (hexA || "#888").replace("#", "");
  const cb = (hexB || "#888").replace("#", "");
  const ar = parseInt(ca.substring(0,2), 16), ag = parseInt(ca.substring(2,4), 16), ab = parseInt(ca.substring(4,6), 16);
  const br = parseInt(cb.substring(0,2), 16), bg = parseInt(cb.substring(2,4), 16), bb = parseInt(cb.substring(4,6), 16);
  const r = Math.round(ar + (br - ar) * t);
  const g = Math.round(ag + (bg - ag) * t);
  const b = Math.round(ab + (bb - ab) * t);
  return `rgb(${r},${g},${b})`;
}

/* ============================================================
   BIOME SCATTER RENDER — draws the per-match decorative sprites
   (grass tufts, dandelions, pebbles, ice cracks, embers, petals)
   directly to the floor canvas. No filtering or culling — the count
   is bounded (<300 sprites typically) so blasting through is fast.
   ============================================================ */
function drawBiomeScatter(scatter) {
  for (const s of scatter) {
    switch (s.type) {
      case "grassTuft": {
        // Three little vertical blades fanning out
        ctx.strokeStyle = s.c1;
        ctx.lineWidth = 1.2;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.moveTo(s.x, s.y + 2);
        ctx.lineTo(s.x, s.y - 4 - s.r);
        ctx.moveTo(s.x - 2, s.y + 2);
        ctx.lineTo(s.x - 3, s.y - 2 - s.r);
        ctx.moveTo(s.x + 2, s.y + 2);
        ctx.lineTo(s.x + 3, s.y - 2 - s.r);
        ctx.stroke();
        // Highlight on the center blade
        ctx.strokeStyle = s.c2;
        ctx.lineWidth = 0.8;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.moveTo(s.x, s.y + 1);
        ctx.lineTo(s.x, s.y - 3 - s.r);
        ctx.stroke();
        break;
      }
      case "dandelion": {
        // Tiny puff: a yellow-ish circle + small white dots around it
        ctx.fillStyle = s.c1;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.arc(s.x, s.y, 1.6, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.fill();
        ctx.fillStyle = s.c2;
        for (let i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
          const a = i * Math.PI / 2 + s.rot;
          ctx.beginPath();
          ctx.arc(s.x + Math.cos(a) * 2.2, s.y + Math.sin(a) * 2.2, 0.6, 0, Math.PI * 2);
          ctx.fill();
        }
        break;
      }
      case "pebble": {
        // Small dark ellipse with highlight
        ctx.fillStyle = s.c1;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.ellipse(s.x, s.y, 1.5 + s.r * 0.5, 1 + s.r * 0.4, s.rot, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.fill();
        ctx.fillStyle = s.c2;
        ctx.fillRect(s.x - 1, s.y - 0.5, 1, 0.5);
        break;
      }
      case "cactus": {
        // Vertical green oval w/ ridge
        ctx.fillStyle = s.c1;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.ellipse(s.x, s.y, 2 + s.r, 5 + s.r * 1.5, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.fill();
        ctx.strokeStyle = s.c2;
        ctx.lineWidth = 0.8;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.moveTo(s.x, s.y - 5 - s.r);
        ctx.lineTo(s.x, s.y + 5 + s.r);
        ctx.stroke();
        break;
      }
      case "sandTuft": {
        // Tiny windswept marks
        ctx.strokeStyle = s.c2;
        ctx.lineWidth = 0.8;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.moveTo(s.x - 2, s.y);
        ctx.lineTo(s.x + 2, s.y - 1);
        ctx.stroke();
        break;
      }
      case "iceCrack": {
        // Jagged crack lines
        ctx.strokeStyle = s.c1;
        ctx.lineWidth = 1;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.moveTo(s.x - 6, s.y);
        ctx.lineTo(s.x - 1, s.y + 2);
        ctx.lineTo(s.x + 3, s.y - 2);
        ctx.lineTo(s.x + 7, s.y + 1);
        ctx.stroke();
        ctx.strokeStyle = s.c2;
        ctx.lineWidth = 0.5;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.moveTo(s.x - 1, s.y + 2);
        ctx.lineTo(s.x + 1, s.y + 5);
        ctx.stroke();
        break;
      }
      case "snowTuft": {
        // Tiny white dot
        ctx.fillStyle = s.c2;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.arc(s.x, s.y, 0.8 + s.r * 0.3, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.fill();
        break;
      }
      case "ember": {
        // Glowing dot with halo
        ctx.fillStyle = _hexA(s.c1.replace(/^#/, "#") || "#ff8030", 0.18);
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.arc(s.x, s.y, 4, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.fill();
        ctx.fillStyle = s.c2;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.arc(s.x, s.y, 1.2, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.fill();
        break;
      }
      case "crack": {
        // Glowing volcanic crack
        ctx.strokeStyle = s.c1;
        ctx.lineWidth = 2.2;
        ctx.lineCap = "round";
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.moveTo(s.x - 8, s.y);
        ctx.lineTo(s.x + 6, s.y + 2);
        ctx.lineTo(s.x + 10, s.y - 1);
        ctx.stroke();
        ctx.strokeStyle = s.c2;
        ctx.lineWidth = 0.8;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.moveTo(s.x - 8, s.y);
        ctx.lineTo(s.x + 6, s.y + 2);
        ctx.stroke();
        ctx.lineCap = "butt";
        break;
      }
      case "petal": {
        // Soft pink ellipse
        ctx.save();
        ctx.translate(s.x, s.y);
        ctx.rotate(s.rot);
        ctx.fillStyle = s.c1;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.ellipse(0, 0, 2.8, 1.5, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.fill();
        ctx.fillStyle = s.c2;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.ellipse(-0.4, -0.4, 1.4, 0.7, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.fill();
        ctx.restore();
        break;
      }
      case "crystal": {
        // Diamond / triangle gem shape — pointy with bright highlight
        ctx.save();
        ctx.translate(s.x, s.y);
        ctx.rotate(s.rot);
        ctx.fillStyle = s.c1;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.moveTo(0, -3 - s.r);
        ctx.lineTo(2.5, 0);
        ctx.lineTo(0, 3 + s.r * 0.6);
        ctx.lineTo(-2.5, 0);
        ctx.closePath();
        ctx.fill();
        ctx.fillStyle = s.c2;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.moveTo(0, -3 - s.r);
        ctx.lineTo(1, 0);
        ctx.lineTo(0, 0);
        ctx.closePath();
        ctx.fill();
        ctx.restore();
        break;
      }
      case "stalagmite": {
        // Pointy upward cone
        ctx.fillStyle = s.c1;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.moveTo(s.x - 4, s.y + 4);
        ctx.lineTo(s.x, s.y - 6 - s.r);
        ctx.lineTo(s.x + 4, s.y + 4);
        ctx.closePath();
        ctx.fill();
        ctx.fillStyle = s.c2;
        ctx.fillRect(s.x - 1, s.y - 5 - s.r, 1, 4);
        break;
      }
      case "seaweed": {
        // Wavy vertical strand
        ctx.strokeStyle = s.c1;
        ctx.lineWidth = 2;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.moveTo(s.x, s.y + 6);
        ctx.bezierCurveTo(s.x - 3, s.y + 2, s.x + 3, s.y - 2, s.x, s.y - 8 - s.r * 2);
        ctx.stroke();
        ctx.strokeStyle = s.c2;
        ctx.lineWidth = 1;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.moveTo(s.x, s.y + 4);
        ctx.bezierCurveTo(s.x - 2, s.y + 1, s.x + 2, s.y - 1, s.x, s.y - 6 - s.r * 2);
        ctx.stroke();
        break;
      }
      case "coral": {
        // Tree-branch shape with multiple prongs
        ctx.fillStyle = s.c1;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.arc(s.x, s.y, 3 + s.r, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.fill();
        ctx.strokeStyle = s.c1;
        ctx.lineWidth = 2;
        ctx.beginPath();
        for (let a = -Math.PI / 2; a < Math.PI / 2; a += Math.PI / 5) {
          ctx.moveTo(s.x, s.y);
          ctx.lineTo(s.x + Math.cos(a) * 5, s.y + Math.sin(a) * 5);
        }
        ctx.stroke();
        ctx.fillStyle = s.c2;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.arc(s.x, s.y, 1.2, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.fill();
        break;
      }
      case "manhole": {
        // Round dark circle with cross pattern
        ctx.fillStyle = s.c1;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.arc(s.x, s.y, 9, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.fill();
        ctx.strokeStyle = s.c2;
        ctx.lineWidth = 1;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.arc(s.x, s.y, 7.5, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.stroke();
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.moveTo(s.x - 6, s.y); ctx.lineTo(s.x + 6, s.y);
        ctx.moveTo(s.x, s.y - 6); ctx.lineTo(s.x, s.y + 6);
        ctx.stroke();
        break;
      }
      case "oilStain": {
        // Irregular dark blob
        ctx.fillStyle = s.c1;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.ellipse(s.x, s.y, 5 + s.r, 3 + s.r * 0.6, s.rot, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.fill();
        ctx.fillStyle = s.c2;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.ellipse(s.x - 1, s.y - 0.5, 2, 1.2, s.rot, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.fill();
        break;
      }
      case "gum": {
        // Tiny squashed circle
        ctx.fillStyle = s.c1;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.ellipse(s.x, s.y, 1.8, 1.2, s.rot, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.fill();
        break;
      }
      case "leaf": {
        // Pointed oval, rotated
        ctx.save();
        ctx.translate(s.x, s.y);
        ctx.rotate(s.rot);
        ctx.fillStyle = s.c1;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.ellipse(0, 0, 3.5 + s.r * 0.4, 1.6, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.fill();
        ctx.strokeStyle = s.c2;
        ctx.lineWidth = 0.6;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.moveTo(-3, 0); ctx.lineTo(3, 0);
        ctx.stroke();
        ctx.restore();
        break;
      }
      case "vine": {
        // Curving thin line
        ctx.strokeStyle = s.c1;
        ctx.lineWidth = 1.2;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.moveTo(s.x - 8, s.y);
        ctx.bezierCurveTo(s.x - 2, s.y - 4, s.x + 2, s.y + 4, s.x + 8, s.y);
        ctx.stroke();
        ctx.strokeStyle = s.c2;
        ctx.lineWidth = 0.6;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.moveTo(s.x, s.y - 1);
        ctx.lineTo(s.x + 3, s.y - 4);
        ctx.stroke();
        break;
      }
      case "gridDot": {
        // Bright glowing dot — neon biomes
        ctx.fillStyle = _hexA(s.c1.startsWith("#") ? s.c1 : "#ff40c8", 0.20);
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.arc(s.x, s.y, 3, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.fill();
        ctx.fillStyle = s.c1;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.arc(s.x, s.y, 1, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.fill();
        ctx.fillStyle = "#fff";
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.arc(s.x, s.y, 0.4, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.fill();
        break;
      }
      case "neonStripe": {
        // Short bright horizontal line with glow
        ctx.save();
        ctx.translate(s.x, s.y);
        ctx.rotate(s.rot);
        ctx.strokeStyle = _hexA(s.c1.startsWith("#") ? s.c1 : "#ff40c8", 0.30);
        ctx.lineWidth = 4;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.moveTo(-10, 0); ctx.lineTo(10, 0);
        ctx.stroke();
        ctx.strokeStyle = s.c2;
        ctx.lineWidth = 1;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.moveTo(-10, 0); ctx.lineTo(10, 0);
        ctx.stroke();
        ctx.restore();
        break;
      }
      case "glowMushroom": {
        // Small mushroom with bright cap
        ctx.fillStyle = "#3a3028";
        ctx.fillRect(s.x - 0.7, s.y - 1, 1.4, 4);
        ctx.fillStyle = _hexA(s.c1.startsWith("#") ? s.c1 : "#88e0c8", 0.4);
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.arc(s.x, s.y - 1, 4, Math.PI, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.fill();
        ctx.fillStyle = s.c1;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.arc(s.x, s.y - 1, 2.5, Math.PI, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.fill();
        ctx.fillStyle = s.c2;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.arc(s.x, s.y - 1.5, 0.7, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.fill();
        break;
      }
      case "evergreen": {
        // Triangular pine tree silhouette
        ctx.fillStyle = "rgba(0,0,0,0.30)";
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.ellipse(s.x + 2, s.y + 8, 8, 2, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.fill();
        ctx.fillStyle = s.c1;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.moveTo(s.x - 6, s.y + 6);
        ctx.lineTo(s.x, s.y - 12);
        ctx.lineTo(s.x + 6, s.y + 6);
        ctx.closePath();
        ctx.fill();
        ctx.fillStyle = s.c2;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.moveTo(s.x - 4, s.y + 2);
        ctx.lineTo(s.x, s.y - 8);
        ctx.lineTo(s.x + 4, s.y + 2);
        ctx.closePath();
        ctx.fill();
        ctx.fillStyle = "#3d2515";
        ctx.fillRect(s.x - 1, s.y + 6, 2, 2);
        break;
      }
      case "flowerPink":
      case "flowerYellow": {
        // 5-petal flower with center
        ctx.fillStyle = s.c1;
        for (let i = 0; i < 5; i++) {
          const a = i * (Math.PI * 2 / 5) + s.rot;
          ctx.beginPath();
          ctx.arc(s.x + Math.cos(a) * 1.8, s.y + Math.sin(a) * 1.8, 1.4, 0, Math.PI * 2);
          ctx.fill();
        }
        ctx.fillStyle = s.c2;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.arc(s.x, s.y, 1, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.fill();
        break;
      }
      case "gift": {
        // Wrapped gift box — small square with a ribbon
        ctx.fillStyle = s.c1;
        ctx.fillRect(s.x - 4, s.y - 4, 8, 8);
        ctx.fillStyle = s.c2;
        ctx.fillRect(s.x - 4, s.y - 0.5, 8, 1);
        ctx.fillRect(s.x - 0.5, s.y - 4, 1, 8);
        ctx.fillStyle = s.c2;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.arc(s.x, s.y, 1.4, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.fill();
        break;
      }
      case "ornament": {
        // Christmas ornament — gold/silver bauble with a tiny cap
        ctx.fillStyle = _hexA(s.c1.startsWith("#") ? s.c1 : "#ffc848", 0.35);
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.arc(s.x, s.y, 4.5, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.fill();
        ctx.fillStyle = s.c1;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.arc(s.x, s.y, 3, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.fill();
        ctx.fillStyle = s.c2;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.arc(s.x - 0.8, s.y - 0.8, 1, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.fill();
        ctx.fillStyle = "#5a4830";
        ctx.fillRect(s.x - 0.5, s.y - 4, 1, 1.5);
        break;
      }
      case "pumpkin": {
        // Orange ridged pumpkin
        ctx.fillStyle = "rgba(0,0,0,0.30)";
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.ellipse(s.x + 1, s.y + 4, 5, 1.5, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.fill();
        ctx.fillStyle = s.c1;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.ellipse(s.x, s.y, 5, 4, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.fill();
        ctx.strokeStyle = s.c2;
        ctx.lineWidth = 0.6;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.moveTo(s.x - 2, s.y - 3); ctx.lineTo(s.x - 2, s.y + 3);
        ctx.moveTo(s.x, s.y - 4);     ctx.lineTo(s.x, s.y + 4);
        ctx.moveTo(s.x + 2, s.y - 3); ctx.lineTo(s.x + 2, s.y + 3);
        ctx.stroke();
        // Stem
        ctx.fillStyle = "#3a4818";
        ctx.fillRect(s.x - 0.5, s.y - 5, 1, 2);
        break;
      }
      case "gravestone": {
        // Rounded-top tombstone
        ctx.fillStyle = "rgba(0,0,0,0.40)";
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.ellipse(s.x + 1, s.y + 7, 6, 1.5, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.fill();
        ctx.fillStyle = s.c1;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.moveTo(s.x - 4, s.y + 6);
        ctx.lineTo(s.x - 4, s.y - 3);
        ctx.arc(s.x, s.y - 3, 4, Math.PI, 0, false);
        ctx.lineTo(s.x + 4, s.y + 6);
        ctx.closePath();
        ctx.fill();
        ctx.fillStyle = s.c2;
        ctx.fillRect(s.x - 2.5, s.y - 1, 5, 0.6);
        ctx.fillRect(s.x - 2,   s.y + 1, 4, 0.6);
        ctx.fillRect(s.x - 2.5, s.y + 3, 5, 0.6);
        break;
      }
    }
  }
}

/* ============================================================
   COURT-SPECIFIC FIELD MARKINGS — for tennis/badminton/basketball/
   volleyball biomes. Drawn in place of the generic grid + center
   circle when biome.courtType is set.

   The court runs the full arena, scaled to the available space.
   Lines color comes from biome.accents.lineColor (typically white).
   ============================================================ */
function drawCourtMarkings(W, H, courtType, lineColor) {
  ctx.strokeStyle = lineColor;
  ctx.lineWidth = 3;
  // Outer boundary — common to all courts
  ctx.strokeRect(40, 40, W - 80, H - 80);
  // Center net line (vertical)
  ctx.lineWidth = 4;
  ctx.beginPath();
  ctx.moveTo(W / 2, 30); ctx.lineTo(W / 2, H - 30);
  ctx.stroke();
  ctx.lineWidth = 2;

  if (courtType === "tennis") {
    // Service boxes
    const sx = W / 2 - 200, ex = W / 2 + 200;
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.moveTo(sx, 40);   ctx.lineTo(sx, H - 40);
    ctx.moveTo(ex, 40);   ctx.lineTo(ex, H - 40);
    ctx.moveTo(W / 2, H / 2 - 0); ctx.lineTo(W / 2, H / 2 - 0);
    ctx.moveTo(sx, H / 2);  ctx.lineTo(ex, H / 2);
    ctx.stroke();
    // Singles sidelines
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.moveTo(80, 40);    ctx.lineTo(80, H - 40);
    ctx.moveTo(W - 80, 40); ctx.lineTo(W - 80, H - 40);
    ctx.stroke();
  } else if (courtType === "basketball") {
    // Three-point arc on each side + key (free-throw rectangle)
    const arcR = Math.min(W, H) * 0.32;
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.arc(40, H / 2, arcR, -Math.PI / 2.6, Math.PI / 2.6);
    ctx.stroke();
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.arc(W - 40, H / 2, arcR, Math.PI - Math.PI / 2.6, Math.PI + Math.PI / 2.6);
    ctx.stroke();
    // Key boxes
    ctx.strokeRect(40, H / 2 - 80, 200, 160);
    ctx.strokeRect(W - 240, H / 2 - 80, 200, 160);
    // Free throw circles
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.arc(240, H / 2, 60, 0, Math.PI * 2);
    ctx.stroke();
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.arc(W - 240, H / 2, 60, 0, Math.PI * 2);
    ctx.stroke();
    // Center circle
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.arc(W / 2, H / 2, 60, 0, Math.PI * 2);
    ctx.stroke();
  } else if (courtType === "volleyball") {
    // Attack lines 3m from net
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.moveTo(W / 2 - 180, 40);   ctx.lineTo(W / 2 - 180, H - 40);
    ctx.moveTo(W / 2 + 180, 40);   ctx.lineTo(W / 2 + 180, H - 40);
    ctx.stroke();
    // Net dotted line down center
    ctx.setLineDash([10, 6]);
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.moveTo(W / 2, 30); ctx.lineTo(W / 2, H - 30);
    ctx.stroke();
    ctx.setLineDash([]);
  } else if (courtType === "badminton") {
    // Singles sidelines + service lines
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.moveTo(70, 40);    ctx.lineTo(70, H - 40);
    ctx.moveTo(W - 70, 40); ctx.lineTo(W - 70, H - 40);
    ctx.stroke();
    // Short service lines (close to net)
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.moveTo(W / 2 - 100, 40);   ctx.lineTo(W / 2 - 100, H - 40);
    ctx.moveTo(W / 2 + 100, 40);   ctx.lineTo(W / 2 + 100, H - 40);
    ctx.stroke();
    // Center service line
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.moveTo(W / 2 - 100, H / 2); ctx.lineTo(W / 2 + 100, H / 2);
    ctx.stroke();
  }
}

/* ============================================================
   CLOUD SHADOWS — soft semi-transparent dark patches that drift
   diagonally across the field. Drawn beneath the vignette so they
   read as actual cloud cover rather than a UI element.
   ============================================================ */
function drawCloudShadows(clouds) {
  for (const c of clouds) {
    const grd = ctx.createRadialGradient(
      c.x + c.w / 2, c.y + c.h / 2, 0,
      c.x + c.w / 2, c.y + c.h / 2, Math.max(c.w, c.h) / 2
    );
    grd.addColorStop(0, `rgba(0,0,0,${c.alpha})`);
    grd.addColorStop(0.7, `rgba(0,0,0,${c.alpha * 0.5})`);
    grd.addColorStop(1, "rgba(0,0,0,0)");
    ctx.fillStyle = grd;
    ctx.fillRect(c.x, c.y, c.w, c.h);
  }
}

/* ============================================================
   STADIUM PITCH LIGHTS — soft warm flares in the four corners.
   Sells the broadcast-stadium fiction. Drawn AFTER the vignette
   so the lights cut through the dark corners. Each flare is a
   radial gradient from warm white at the corner edge to fully
   transparent at ~25% of the diagonal. Subtle but present.
   ============================================================ */
function drawPitchLights(W, H) {
  const flareR = Math.min(W, H) * 0.42;
  const corners = [
    [0,     0    ],   // top-left
    [W,     0    ],   // top-right
    [0,     H    ],   // bottom-left
    [W,     H    ],   // bottom-right
  ];
  for (const [cx, cy] of corners) {
    const g = ctx.createRadialGradient(cx, cy, 0, cx, cy, flareR);
    g.addColorStop(0,   "rgba(255,240,210,0.18)");
    g.addColorStop(0.4, "rgba(255,235,200,0.06)");
    g.addColorStop(1,   "rgba(255,235,200,0)");
    ctx.fillStyle = g;
    ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
  }
  // Tiny "lamp" pips in each corner so the lights have a visible source
  for (const [cx, cy] of corners) {
    const px = cx === 0 ? 8 : W - 8;
    const py = cy === 0 ? 8 : H - 8;
    ctx.fillStyle = "rgba(255,250,230,0.6)";
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.arc(px, py, 2.2, 0, Math.PI * 2);
    ctx.fill();
    ctx.fillStyle = "rgba(255,255,255,1)";
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.arc(px, py, 1.0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
    ctx.fill();
  }
}

/* ============================================================
   AMBIENT PARTICLES — biome-driven atmospheric layer drawn
   between cloud shadows and the vignette so they read as airborne
   particles in front of the field but behind the broadcast lighting.

   - snow: tiny white dot with soft halo
   - ember: glowing orange pip with halo + life-fade
   - petal: rotated soft pink ellipse (with highlight)
   - shimmer: vertical wobble line, very faint (heat distortion)
   ============================================================ */
function drawAmbientParticles(particles) {
  for (const p of particles) {
    if (p.type === "snow") {
      ctx.fillStyle = "rgba(255,255,255,0.18)";
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.arc(p.x, p.y, p.r * 1.6, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      ctx.fill();
      ctx.fillStyle = "rgba(255,255,255,0.95)";
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.arc(p.x, p.y, p.r, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      ctx.fill();
    } else if (p.type === "ember") {
      // Life-fade — particle dims as it rises
      const a = clamp(p.life / 80, 0.05, 0.85);
      ctx.fillStyle = `rgba(255,140,40,${a * 0.30})`;
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.arc(p.x, p.y, p.r * 2.5, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      ctx.fill();
      ctx.fillStyle = `rgba(255,200,80,${a})`;
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.arc(p.x, p.y, p.r, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      ctx.fill();
    } else if (p.type === "petal") {
      ctx.save();
      ctx.translate(p.x, p.y);
      ctx.rotate(p.rot);
      ctx.fillStyle = "rgba(255,176,200,0.85)";
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.ellipse(0, 0, p.r * 1.6, p.r * 0.7, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      ctx.fill();
      ctx.fillStyle = "rgba(255,210,225,0.95)";
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.ellipse(-p.r * 0.3, -p.r * 0.2, p.r * 0.7, p.r * 0.3, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      ctx.fill();
      ctx.restore();
    } else if (p.type === "shimmer") {
      // Heat shimmer — short vertical wobble line, very faint
      const sway = Math.sin(p.phase) * 1.5;
      ctx.strokeStyle = "rgba(255,220,180,0.10)";
      ctx.lineWidth = 1;
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.moveTo(p.x + sway, p.y);
      ctx.lineTo(p.x - sway, p.y + 5);
      ctx.stroke();
    } else if (p.type === "bubble") {
      // Underwater bubble — circle outline with a highlight pip
      ctx.strokeStyle = "rgba(220,240,255,0.55)";
      ctx.lineWidth = 1;
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.arc(p.x, p.y, p.r, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      ctx.stroke();
      ctx.fillStyle = "rgba(255,255,255,0.65)";
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.arc(p.x - p.r * 0.4, p.y - p.r * 0.4, p.r * 0.3, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      ctx.fill();
    } else if (p.type === "leaf") {
      // Larger drifting leaf — different from the static scatter leaves
      ctx.save();
      ctx.translate(p.x, p.y);
      ctx.rotate(p.rot);
      ctx.fillStyle = "rgba(216,96,40,0.85)";
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.ellipse(0, 0, p.r * 1.8, p.r * 0.9, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      ctx.fill();
      ctx.strokeStyle = "rgba(120,52,18,0.6)";
      ctx.lineWidth = 0.5;
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.moveTo(-p.r * 1.8, 0); ctx.lineTo(p.r * 1.8, 0);
      ctx.stroke();
      ctx.restore();
    } else if (p.type === "firefly") {
      // Pulsing yellow-green dot with halo
      const pulse = 0.5 + Math.sin(p.phase) * 0.5;
      const a = clamp(p.life / 200, 0.3, 1);
      ctx.fillStyle = `rgba(180,255,120,${pulse * 0.30 * a})`;
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.arc(p.x, p.y, 4, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      ctx.fill();
      ctx.fillStyle = `rgba(220,255,140,${pulse * 0.95 * a})`;
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.arc(p.x, p.y, 1.4, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      ctx.fill();
    } else if (p.type === "spark") {
      // Bright neon trail — short streak with glow
      const a = clamp(p.life / 30, 0, 1);
      const isMagenta = p.hue === "magenta";
      const c1 = isMagenta ? "255,80,200" : "80,224,255";
      ctx.strokeStyle = `rgba(${c1},${0.30 * a})`;
      ctx.lineWidth = 3;
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.moveTo(p.x - p.vx * 3, p.y - p.vy * 3);
      ctx.lineTo(p.x, p.y);
      ctx.stroke();
      ctx.strokeStyle = `rgba(255,255,255,${0.95 * a})`;
      ctx.lineWidth = 1;
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.moveTo(p.x - p.vx * 2, p.y - p.vy * 2);
      ctx.lineTo(p.x, p.y);
      ctx.stroke();
    } else if (p.type === "bat") {
      // Bat silhouette — wing flap via phase
      const wingY = Math.sin(p.phase) * 2;
      ctx.fillStyle = "rgba(20,15,25,0.85)";
      ctx.beginPath();
      // Body
      ctx.ellipse(p.x, p.y, 2, 1.5, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      ctx.fill();
      // Wings — left + right with flap
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.moveTo(p.x, p.y);
      ctx.quadraticCurveTo(p.x - 4, p.y + wingY, p.x - 7, p.y + wingY * 0.5);
      ctx.quadraticCurveTo(p.x - 5, p.y + 1, p.x, p.y);
      ctx.fill();
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.moveTo(p.x, p.y);
      ctx.quadraticCurveTo(p.x + 4, p.y + wingY, p.x + 7, p.y + wingY * 0.5);
      ctx.quadraticCurveTo(p.x + 5, p.y + 1, p.x, p.y);
      ctx.fill();
    } else if (p.type === "drip") {
      // Cave water drip — small teardrop, brightens as it falls
      ctx.fillStyle = "rgba(120,200,255,0.35)";
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.ellipse(p.x, p.y, 0.8, 1.6, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      ctx.fill();
      ctx.fillStyle = "rgba(255,255,255,0.7)";
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.arc(p.x, p.y - 0.3, 0.4, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      ctx.fill();
    }
  }
}

function drawScenery() {
  for (const s of SCENERY) {
    if (s.type === "pillar") {
      // Stone pillar with shadow
      ctx.fillStyle = "rgba(0,0,0,0.35)";
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.ellipse(s.x + 2, s.y + 14, 8, 3, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      ctx.fill();
      ctx.fillStyle = "#3a4555";
      ctx.fillRect(s.x - 6, s.y - 12, 12, 24);
      ctx.fillStyle = "#4d5a6c";
      ctx.fillRect(s.x - 6, s.y - 12, 12, 3);
      ctx.fillRect(s.x - 6, s.y + 9,  12, 3);
      ctx.fillStyle = "#2a323d";
      ctx.fillRect(s.x - 7, s.y - 14, 14, 2);
      ctx.fillRect(s.x - 7, s.y + 12, 14, 2);
    } else if (s.type === "banner") {
      ctx.fillStyle = "#2a323d";
      ctx.fillRect(s.x - 1, s.y - 14, 2, 28);
      ctx.fillStyle = "#7a2828";
      ctx.fillRect(s.x - 8, s.y - 8, 16, 12);
      ctx.fillStyle = "#f7c948";
      ctx.fillRect(s.x - 2, s.y - 4, 4, 4);
    } else if (s.type === "rubble") {
      ctx.fillStyle = "#2a323d";
      ctx.fillRect(s.x - 4, s.y, 8, 4);
      ctx.fillStyle = "#3a4555";
      ctx.fillRect(s.x - 6, s.y + 2, 4, 3);
      ctx.fillRect(s.x + 2, s.y + 1, 4, 3);
    }
  }
}

function drawDrops(m) {
  for (const d of m.drops) {
    const bob = Math.sin((m.frame + d.x) * 0.12) * 1.5;
    const legendary = (d.gear.scavengedCount || 0) >= 3;

    if (d.slot === "health") {
      // Pulsing red cross — visually distinct from gear drops
      const pulse = 0.7 + 0.3 * Math.sin(m.frame * 0.18 + d.x);
      ctx.fillStyle = `rgba(239,77,77,${0.25 * pulse})`;
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.arc(d.x, d.y + bob, 11, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      ctx.fill();
      // White cross plate
      ctx.fillStyle = "#fff";
      ctx.fillRect(d.x - 6, d.y - 6 + bob, 12, 12);
      ctx.fillStyle = "#ef4d4d";
      ctx.fillRect(d.x - 5, d.y - 1 + bob, 10, 2); // horizontal bar
      ctx.fillRect(d.x - 1, d.y - 5 + bob, 2, 10); // vertical bar
      continue;
    }

    // Gear drops (weapon/armor/item)
    ctx.fillStyle = legendary ? "rgba(180,109,240,0.35)" : "rgba(247,201,72,0.2)";
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.arc(d.x, d.y + bob, legendary ? 11 : 9, 0, Math.PI * 2);
    ctx.fill();
    const bgColor =
      d.slot === "weapon" ? "#5a3030" :
      d.slot === "armor"  ? "#304a5a" : "#3a5a30";
    ctx.fillStyle = bgColor;
    ctx.fillRect(d.x - 5, d.y - 5 + bob, 10, 10);
    if (legendary) {
      ctx.strokeStyle = "#b46df0";
      ctx.lineWidth = 1;
      ctx.strokeRect(d.x - 6, d.y - 6 + bob, 12, 12);
    }
    ctx.fillStyle = "#fff";
    ctx.font = "9px ui-sans-serif, system-ui";
    ctx.textAlign = "center";
    ctx.textBaseline = "middle";
    ctx.fillText(d.gear.ico || "?", d.x, d.y + bob);
    ctx.textBaseline = "alphabetic";
  }
}

function drawProjectiles(m) {
  if (!m.projectiles) return;
  for (const pr of m.projectiles) {
    const team = TEAM_BY_ID[pr.teamId];
    const isRailgun = pr.wpn?.id === "railgun";
    if (isRailgun) {
      // Long bright railgun streak — multi-segment trail with team color
      ctx.strokeStyle = `rgba(255,255,255,0.65)`;
      ctx.lineWidth = 2;
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.moveTo(pr.x - pr.vx * 4.0, pr.y - pr.vy * 4.0);
      ctx.lineTo(pr.x, pr.y);
      ctx.stroke();
      // Team-colored outer glow
      ctx.strokeStyle = team.color;
      ctx.lineWidth = 1;
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.moveTo(pr.x - pr.vx * 3.0, pr.y - pr.vy * 3.0);
      ctx.lineTo(pr.x, pr.y);
      ctx.stroke();
      // Bright head
      ctx.fillStyle = "#fff";
      ctx.fillRect(pr.x - 1, pr.y - 1, 3, 3);
    } else {
      // Laser pistol — fading 4-segment trail
      const tx = pr.vx, ty = pr.vy;
      // Outer dim halo
      ctx.fillStyle = `rgba(239,77,77,0.18)`;
      ctx.fillRect(pr.x - tx * 2.4 - 2, pr.y - ty * 2.4 - 2, 4, 4);
      // Mid trail
      ctx.fillStyle = `rgba(239,77,77,0.45)`;
      ctx.fillRect(pr.x - tx * 1.4 - 1, pr.y - ty * 1.4 - 1, 3, 3);
      // Bright body
      ctx.fillStyle = "#ef4d4d";
      ctx.fillRect(pr.x - tx * 0.6 - 1, pr.y - ty * 0.6 - 1, 3, 3);
      // White-hot core
      ctx.fillStyle = "#fff";
      ctx.fillRect(pr.x - 1, pr.y - 1, 2, 2);
    }
  }
}

function drawTerrain(m) {
  if (!m.terrain) return;
  for (const t of m.terrain) {
    ctx.fillStyle = "rgba(0,0,0,0.5)";
    ctx.fillRect(t.x + 2, t.y + 2, t.size, t.size);
    ctx.fillStyle = "#3a4555";
    ctx.fillRect(t.x, t.y, t.size, t.size);
    ctx.fillStyle = "#5a6678";
    ctx.fillRect(t.x, t.y, t.size, 4);
    ctx.fillRect(t.x, t.y, 4, t.size);
    ctx.fillStyle = "#2a323d";
    ctx.fillRect(t.x + t.size - 4, t.y, 4, t.size);
    ctx.fillRect(t.x, t.y + t.size - 4, t.size, 4);
    ctx.fillStyle = "#1f2630";
    ctx.fillRect(t.x + t.size / 2 - 1, t.y + 4, 2, t.size - 8);
    ctx.fillRect(t.x + 4, t.y + t.size / 2 - 1, t.size - 8, 2);
  }
}

function drawPowerCore(m) {
  if (!m.powerCore || m.powerCore.state !== "active") return;
  const pc = m.powerCore;
  const t = m.frame;
  const pulse = 0.7 + 0.3 * Math.sin(t * 0.15);
  const grd = ctx.createRadialGradient(pc.x, pc.y, 4, pc.x, pc.y, 30);
  grd.addColorStop(0, `rgba(247,201,72,${0.55 * pulse})`);
  grd.addColorStop(1, "rgba(247,201,72,0)");
  ctx.fillStyle = grd;
  ctx.fillRect(pc.x - 30, pc.y - 30, 60, 60);
  for (let i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
    const ang = (t * 0.05) + (i * Math.PI / 2);
    const x1 = pc.x + Math.cos(ang) * 8, y1 = pc.y + Math.sin(ang) * 8;
    const x2 = pc.x + Math.cos(ang) * 14, y2 = pc.y + Math.sin(ang) * 14;
    ctx.strokeStyle = `rgba(247,201,72,${pulse})`;
    ctx.lineWidth = 2;
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.moveTo(x1, y1);
    ctx.lineTo(x2, y2);
    ctx.stroke();
  }
  ctx.fillStyle = "#fff";
  ctx.beginPath();
  ctx.arc(pc.x, pc.y, 5, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  ctx.fill();
  ctx.fillStyle = `rgba(247,201,72,${pulse})`;
  ctx.beginPath();
  ctx.arc(pc.x, pc.y, 3, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  ctx.fill();
  ctx.fillStyle = "rgba(247,201,72,0.9)";
  ctx.font = "bold 9px ui-sans-serif, system-ui";
  ctx.textAlign = "center";
  ctx.textBaseline = "middle";
  ctx.fillText("⚡POWER", pc.x, pc.y - 22);
  ctx.textBaseline = "alphabetic";
}

/* ============================================================
   CANVAS RENDERER
   Pixel-art arena: turf, scenery, players (team-colored knights
   with balloon HP), blood, corpses, drops, floating dmg numbers.
   ============================================================ */
function drawArena() {
  const W = arenaCanvas.width, H = arenaCanvas.height;
  const m = LEAGUE.match;
  const biome = m && m.biome ? m.biome : null;

  // === FLOOR === Biome-driven base + secondary gradient
  const topColor = biome ? biome.floorTop : "#0d1018";
  const botColor = biome ? biome.floorBot : "#1a232b";
  const floorGrd = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, 0, 0, H);
  floorGrd.addColorStop(0, topColor);
  floorGrd.addColorStop(0.4, _blend(topColor, botColor, 0.5));
  floorGrd.addColorStop(1, botColor);
  ctx.fillStyle = floorGrd;
  ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);

  // Mowing stripes — alternating bands across the field, classic stadium
  // pitch look. The stripeHue color is layered on top so it works against
  // any base. 60px-wide stripes feel right for the 1600x720 arena.
  if (biome) {
    const stripeW = 60;
    ctx.fillStyle = biome.stripeHue;
    for (let x = 0; x < W; x += stripeW * 2) {
      ctx.fillRect(x, 0, stripeW, H);
    }
  }

  // Team-colored end zones — left third tinted teamA color, right third teamB
  if (m && m.teamAId && m.teamBId) {
    const ta = TEAM_BY_ID[m.teamAId], tb = TEAM_BY_ID[m.teamBId];
    if (ta) {
      const lg = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, 0, W * 0.3, 0);
      lg.addColorStop(0, _hexA(ta.color, 0.18));
      lg.addColorStop(1, "rgba(0,0,0,0)");
      ctx.fillStyle = lg;
      ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W * 0.3, H);
    }
    if (tb) {
      const rg = ctx.createLinearGradient(W * 0.7, 0, W, 0);
      rg.addColorStop(0, "rgba(0,0,0,0)");
      rg.addColorStop(1, _hexA(tb.color, 0.18));
      ctx.fillStyle = rg;
      ctx.fillRect(W * 0.7, 0, W * 0.3, H);
    }
  }

  // === DECORATIVE SCATTER === drawn on top of the floor, beneath everything
  // else. Each sprite is a tiny shape using the biome's palette.
  if (m && m.scatter) {
    drawBiomeScatter(m.scatter);
  }

  // Field lines — biome-aware accent color
  const lineColor = biome ? biome.accents.lineColor : "rgba(247,201,72,0.06)";
  const brandColor = biome ? biome.accents.brandColor : "rgba(247,201,72,0.12)";

  // === FIELD MARKINGS ===
  // For sport-court biomes (tennis, basketball, etc), draw court-specific
  // line geometry via drawCourtMarkings. Otherwise draw the generic
  // mowing-stripe grid + center circle + penalty arcs.
  if (biome && biome.courtType) {
    drawCourtMarkings(W, H, biome.courtType, lineColor);
    // Brand mark + team mottos still go on top
    if (m && m.teamAId && m.teamBId) {
      const ta = TEAM_BY_ID[m.teamAId], tb = TEAM_BY_ID[m.teamBId];
      if (ta) {
        ctx.save();
        ctx.fillStyle = _hexA(ta.color, 0.22);
        ctx.font = "bold 18px ui-sans-serif, system-ui";
        ctx.textAlign = "center";
        ctx.textBaseline = "middle";
        ctx.translate(110, H/2);
        ctx.rotate(-Math.PI/2);
        ctx.fillText((ta.short || "").toUpperCase(), 0, 0);
        ctx.restore();
      }
      if (tb) {
        ctx.save();
        ctx.fillStyle = _hexA(tb.color, 0.22);
        ctx.font = "bold 18px ui-sans-serif, system-ui";
        ctx.textAlign = "center";
        ctx.textBaseline = "middle";
        ctx.translate(W - 110, H/2);
        ctx.rotate(Math.PI/2);
        ctx.fillText((tb.short || "").toUpperCase(), 0, 0);
        ctx.restore();
      }
    }
    ctx.fillStyle = brandColor;
    ctx.font = "bold 38px ui-sans-serif, system-ui";
    ctx.textAlign = "center";
    ctx.textBaseline = "middle";
    ctx.fillText("FK", W/2, H/2);
    ctx.textBaseline = "alphabetic";
    ctx.textAlign = "left";
  } else {
    // Standard arena field markings (the v21 layout)
    // Subtle grid (much fainter when biome has decorations)
    ctx.strokeStyle = biome ? "rgba(255,255,255,0.04)" : "rgba(247,201,72,0.06)";
    ctx.lineWidth = 1;
    for (let i = 0; i < W; i += 60) {
      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(i, 0); ctx.lineTo(i, H); ctx.stroke();
    }
    for (let i = 0; i < H; i += 60) {
      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(0, i); ctx.lineTo(W, i); ctx.stroke();
    }

    ctx.strokeStyle = lineColor;
    ctx.lineWidth = 2.5;
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.moveTo(W/2, 0); ctx.lineTo(W/2, H); ctx.stroke();

    ctx.lineWidth = 2.5;
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(W/2, H/2, 70, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.stroke();
    ctx.strokeStyle = brandColor;
    ctx.lineWidth = 1.5;
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(W/2, H/2, 55, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.stroke();

    ctx.strokeStyle = lineColor;
    ctx.lineWidth = 2;
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.arc(0, H/2, 90, -Math.PI/2, Math.PI/2);
    ctx.stroke();
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.arc(W, H/2, 90, Math.PI/2, -Math.PI/2);
    ctx.stroke();

    if (m && m.teamAId && m.teamBId) {
      const ta = TEAM_BY_ID[m.teamAId], tb = TEAM_BY_ID[m.teamBId];
      if (ta) {
        ctx.save();
        ctx.fillStyle = _hexA(ta.color, 0.22);
        ctx.font = "bold 18px ui-sans-serif, system-ui";
        ctx.textAlign = "center";
        ctx.textBaseline = "middle";
        ctx.translate(110, H/2);
        ctx.rotate(-Math.PI/2);
        ctx.fillText((ta.short || "").toUpperCase(), 0, 0);
        ctx.restore();
      }
      if (tb) {
        ctx.save();
        ctx.fillStyle = _hexA(tb.color, 0.22);
        ctx.font = "bold 18px ui-sans-serif, system-ui";
        ctx.textAlign = "center";
        ctx.textBaseline = "middle";
        ctx.translate(W - 110, H/2);
        ctx.rotate(Math.PI/2);
        ctx.fillText((tb.short || "").toUpperCase(), 0, 0);
        ctx.restore();
      }
    }

    ctx.fillStyle = brandColor;
    ctx.font = "bold 38px ui-sans-serif, system-ui";
    ctx.textAlign = "center";
    ctx.textBaseline = "middle";
    ctx.fillText("FK", W/2, H/2);
    ctx.textBaseline = "alphabetic";
    ctx.textAlign = "left";
  }

  // === CLOUD SHADOWS === drift across the floor, beneath players. Subtle
  // soft-edged dark patches that move slowly. Drawn before the vignette so
  // they don't interfere with the broadcast lighting feel.
  // Skipped when the user has disabled field FX.
  const fxOff = LEAGUE_CONFIG.disableFieldFx;
  const lightOff = LEAGUE_CONFIG.disableLighting;
  if (m && m.clouds && !fxOff) {
    drawCloudShadows(m.clouds);
  }

  // === AMBIENT PARTICLES === biome-specific airborne effect (snow, embers,
  // petals, shimmer). Drawn after clouds so they appear in front of the
  // shadows, before the vignette so the broadcast lighting still rims them.
  if (m && m.ambient && m.ambient.length && !fxOff) {
    drawAmbientParticles(m.ambient);
  }

  // Sky tint vignette around edges (broadcast lighting). Biome controls
  // the warmth of the corners — orange for desert, blue for ice, etc.
  // Skipped when the user has disabled lighting.
  if (!lightOff) {
    const vignetteColor = biome ? biome.skyTint : "rgba(0,0,0,0.4)";
    const vg = ctx.createRadialGradient(W/2, H/2, Math.min(W,H) * 0.4, W/2, H/2, Math.max(W,H) * 0.7);
    vg.addColorStop(0, "rgba(0,0,0,0)");
    vg.addColorStop(1, vignetteColor);
    ctx.fillStyle = vg;
    ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
  }

  // === STADIUM PITCH LIGHTS ===
  // Soft warm flares in each corner — sells the broadcast-stadium fiction.
  // Drawn AFTER the vignette so the lights cut through the dark corners.
  // Each is a radial gradient from warm white at the corner to transparent.
  if (!lightOff) {
    drawPitchLights(W, H);
  }

  drawScenery();

  if (!LEAGUE.match) {
    ctx.fillStyle = "#5a6675";
    ctx.font = "16px ui-sans-serif, system-ui";
    ctx.textAlign = "center";
    ctx.fillText("Awaiting next match…", W/2, H/2 - 100);
    ctx.textAlign = "left";
    return;
  }

  // Blood spatter (under players)
  for (const b of m.blood) {
    ctx.fillStyle = b.color;
    ctx.fillRect(Math.floor(b.x), Math.floor(b.y), 2, 2);
  }
  // Corpse markers
  for (const c of m.corpses) {
    ctx.fillStyle = "#2a2a2a";
    ctx.fillRect(c.x - 5, c.y - 2, 10, 4);
    ctx.fillStyle = c.color;
    ctx.globalAlpha = 0.4;
    ctx.fillRect(c.x - 6, c.y - 6, 12, 12);
    ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
    ctx.strokeStyle = "#000";
    ctx.lineWidth = 1;
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.moveTo(c.x - 4, c.y - 4); ctx.lineTo(c.x + 4, c.y + 4);
    ctx.moveTo(c.x + 4, c.y - 4); ctx.lineTo(c.x - 4, c.y + 4);
    ctx.stroke();
  }

  // Terrain obstacles (drawn over corpses but under drops/players;
  // mascots float OVER them via balloons but bodies clip on the side)
  drawTerrain(m);

  // Power Core (centerpiece objective)
  drawPowerCore(m);

  // Drops (gear lying on the field)
  drawDrops(m);

  // Players — pixel-art mascot knights
  for (const list of [m.teamAplayers, m.teamBplayers]) {
    for (const p of list) {
      if (!p.alive) continue;
      drawMascot(p);
    }
  }

  // Projectiles (drawn over players so they're visible)
  drawProjectiles(m);

  // Floating damage numbers / status pings
  for (const f of m.floats) {
    ctx.fillStyle = f.color;
    ctx.font = "bold 11px 'Courier New', monospace";
    ctx.textAlign = "center";
    ctx.globalAlpha = clamp(f.life / 22, 0, 1);
    ctx.fillText(f.text, f.x, f.y);
    ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
  }
}

function drawMascot(p) {
  const team = TEAM_BY_ID[p.teamId];
  const pos = POS_BY_ID[p.position];
  // Float bob — gentle vertical motion to sell the balloons-on-back fiction
  const t = (LEAGUE.match?.frame || 0);
  const bob = Math.sin((t + p.id * 7) * 0.08) * 1.5;
  const x = Math.floor(p.x);
  const y = Math.floor(p.y + bob);

  // Determine sprite state — driven by the player's current condition.
  // We cache a separate avatar variant per state so transitions look
  // distinct (closed eyes when stunned, wide pupils when on last balloon).
  let state = "alive";
  if (p.stunned > 0) state = "stunned";
  else if ((p.balloonsPopped || 0) >= 2) state = "critical";

  // ----- Drop shadow (on the ground, doesn't bob, soft edged) -----
  // Two-layer: a wider soft halo + tighter inner shadow gives the impression
  // the mascot is genuinely floating above the field. Scaled up to match
  // the ~2x larger sprite footprint.
  if (!LEAGUE_CONFIG.disableLighting) {
    const shadowY = Math.floor(p.y) + 22;
    ctx.fillStyle = "rgba(0,0,0,0.18)";
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.ellipse(x + 1, shadowY, 22, 7, 0, 0, Math.PI*2);
    ctx.fill();
    ctx.fillStyle = "rgba(0,0,0,0.45)";
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.ellipse(x, shadowY, 16, 5, 0, 0, Math.PI*2);
    ctx.fill();
  }

  // ----- Balloons (HP) attached to the helmet, popping at 2/3 and 1/3 -----
  // Larger and positioned higher to clear the bigger sprite. Same physics
  // as before — three balloons, popped[i] hides the i-th one.
  const popped = p.balloonsPopped || 0;
  const balloonY = y - 48;
  const balloonOffsets = [-9, 0, 9];
  ctx.strokeStyle = "rgba(220,220,220,0.6)";
  ctx.lineWidth = 1;
  for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
    if (i < popped) continue;
    const bx = x + balloonOffsets[i];
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.moveTo(x, y - 28);
    ctx.lineTo(bx, balloonY + 5);
    ctx.stroke();
  }
  for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
    if (i < popped) continue;
    const bx = x + balloonOffsets[i];
    ctx.fillStyle = team.color;
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.ellipse(bx, balloonY, 5, 6.5, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
    ctx.fill();
    ctx.fillStyle = "rgba(255,255,255,0.45)";
    ctx.fillRect(bx - 1, balloonY - 3, 2, 2);
    ctx.fillStyle = team.color;
    ctx.fillRect(bx, balloonY + 6, 1, 1);
  }

  // ----- Team-color halo behind the avatar -----
  ctx.fillStyle = team.color;
  ctx.globalAlpha = 0.28;
  ctx.beginPath();
  ctx.arc(x, y, 22, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  ctx.fill();
  ctx.globalAlpha = 1;

  // ----- Avatar sprite (cached) — drawn centered on the player's anchor.
  // The sprite canvas is AVATAR_W × AVATAR_H; the anchor (where the
  // player's logical position lines up) is at (AVATAR_W/2, AVATAR_ANCHOR_Y).
  const sprite = (typeof AVATAR !== "undefined") ? AVATAR.get(p, state) : null;
  if (sprite) {
    // Mirror horizontally based on facing — use save/restore + scale(-1, 1)
    if (p.facing < 0) {
      ctx.save();
      ctx.translate(x, 0);
      ctx.scale(-1, 1);
      ctx.drawImage(sprite, -AVATAR_W / 2, y - AVATAR_ANCHOR_Y);
      ctx.restore();
    } else {
      ctx.drawImage(sprite, x - AVATAR_W / 2, y - AVATAR_ANCHOR_Y);
    }
  } else {
    // Fallback (shouldn't happen in a real browser): emoji rendering, kept
    // here so the function still draws SOMETHING if the avatar canvas fails.
    ctx.font = "16px ui-sans-serif, system-ui, 'Apple Color Emoji', 'Segoe UI Emoji'";
    ctx.textAlign = "center";
    ctx.textBaseline = "middle";
    ctx.fillText("🤖", x, y + 3);
    ctx.font = "14px ui-sans-serif, system-ui, 'Apple Color Emoji', 'Segoe UI Emoji'";
    ctx.fillText(team.emoji, x, y - 9);
  }

  // ----- Weapon icon (held in hand, follows facing) -----
  const wxSign = p.facing >= 0 ? 1 : -1;
  const wpnIco = p.weapon?.ico;
  if (wpnIco) {
    ctx.font = "14px ui-sans-serif, system-ui, 'Apple Color Emoji', 'Segoe UI Emoji'";
    ctx.textAlign = "center";
    ctx.textBaseline = "middle";
    ctx.fillText(wpnIco, x + 18 * wxSign, y + 4);
  }

  // ----- AI state badge (subtle, only for non-default states) -----
  if (p.aiState && p.aiState !== "ENGAGE") {
    const badge =
      p.aiState === "FLEE"       ? "🏃" :
      p.aiState === "SEEK_ITEM"  ? "🎒" :
      p.aiState === "FINISH_OFF" ? "🎯" : "";
    if (badge) {
      ctx.font = "11px ui-sans-serif, system-ui, 'Apple Color Emoji', 'Segoe UI Emoji'";
      ctx.textAlign = "center";
      ctx.textBaseline = "middle";
      ctx.fillText(badge, x + 18, y - 22);
    }
  }

  // ----- Stunned spark (top-of-head pulse) -----
  if (p.stunned > 0) {
    ctx.fillStyle = "#88c0d0";
    ctx.font = "bold 14px 'Courier New', monospace";
    ctx.textAlign = "center";
    ctx.textBaseline = "middle";
    ctx.fillText("⚡", x, y - 38);
  }

  // ----- Boost glow ring -----
  if (p.boostMods) {
    ctx.strokeStyle = "rgba(247,201,72,0.7)";
    ctx.lineWidth = 1.5;
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.arc(x, y, 26, 0, Math.PI * 2);
    ctx.stroke();
  }

  // ----- Attack flicker (white outline pulse just after firing) -----
  if (p.attackCooldown > 12) {
    ctx.strokeStyle = "rgba(255,255,255,0.5)";
    ctx.lineWidth = 1;
    ctx.strokeRect(x - 16, y - 18, 32, 36);
  }

  ctx.textBaseline = "alphabetic";
  ctx.textAlign = "left";
}


function lighten(hex, amt) {
  const c = hex.replace("#", "");
  const r = parseInt(c.substr(0,2), 16);
  const g = parseInt(c.substr(2,2), 16);
  const b = parseInt(c.substr(4,2), 16);
  const f = (v) => clamp(Math.round(v + (255 - v) * amt), 0, 255);
  return `rgb(${f(r)}, ${f(g)}, ${f(b)})`;
}

function darken(hex, amt) {
  const c = hex.replace("#", "");
  const r = parseInt(c.substr(0,2), 16);
  const g = parseInt(c.substr(2,2), 16);
  const b = parseInt(c.substr(4,2), 16);
  const f = (v) => clamp(Math.round(v * (1 - amt)), 0, 255);
  return `rgb(${f(r)}, ${f(g)}, ${f(b)})`;
}

/* ============================================================
   AVATAR SYSTEM — flat-avatar-plugin-style layered mascots.

   The Pablo Stanley flat-avatar approach: compose a character from
   stacked layers (body / face / hair / accessories). For Float
   Knights the equivalent layering is:

       shadow → body+armor → cape (sniper only) → head+ears →
       face markings → eyes → mouth/beak → helmet → number badge

   Implementation:
   - Each (teamId, positionId, state) tuple produces one cached
     offscreen canvas via _renderAvatarTo(). The cache key is a
     simple pipe-delimited string. State ∈ {alive, stunned, critical}.
   - Players within the same team+position share the cached sprite.
     This keeps the cache small (~84 base avatars × 3 states ≈ 252
     canvases worst case) while still letting individuals look
     correct for their archetype.
   - During the live render (drawMascot), we compute the right
     state, look up or lazy-build the sprite, and drawImage it
     centered at (x, y). The previous balloons/halo/state-badges/
     position-color treatments still wrap the sprite, just rescaled
     to the new larger footprint.

   Avatar canvas dimensions: 56x64 logical pixels, drawn 1:1 onto
   the arena (so a player occupies ~56x64 of the 1280x720 logical
   field — about 3x the previous ~16x18 footprint, dramatically
   improving in-game readability).
   ============================================================ */

const AVATAR_W = 56;
const AVATAR_H = 64;
// Center of the rendered head/body — the sprite is anchored at its
// horizontal middle and at the body's vertical center (so the player
// position (p.x, p.y) lines up with the chest).
const AVATAR_ANCHOR_Y = 38; // y within the canvas that the player's (x, y) maps to

const AVATAR = {
  _cache: Object.create(null),

  /**
   * Get a sprite canvas for this player+state, lazily rendering on
   * first request. Returns the offscreen HTMLCanvasElement.
   * Returns null if the environment doesn't support full canvas
   * drawing (e.g. node smoke harness with mock contexts).
   */
  get(player, state) {
    const teamId = player.teamId;
    const posId = player.position;
    const key = teamId + "|" + posId + "|" + state;
    let cached = this._cache[key];
    if (cached !== undefined) return cached;  // cache miss returns null too
    if (typeof document === "undefined") {
      this._cache[key] = null; return null;
    }
    const can = document.createElement("canvas");
    can.width = AVATAR_W;
    can.height = AVATAR_H;
    const cx = (typeof can.getContext === "function") ? can.getContext("2d") : null;
    // Verify all methods we use exist before drawing — some test
    // environments stub createElement to return mocks with partial APIs.
    if (!cx ||
        typeof cx.fillRect          !== "function" ||
        typeof cx.beginPath         !== "function" ||
        typeof cx.ellipse           !== "function" ||
        typeof cx.arc               !== "function" ||
        typeof cx.fill              !== "function" ||
        typeof cx.stroke            !== "function" ||
        typeof cx.moveTo            !== "function" ||
        typeof cx.lineTo            !== "function" ||
        typeof cx.quadraticCurveTo  !== "function" ||
        typeof cx.closePath         !== "function" ||
        typeof cx.clearRect         !== "function") {
      this._cache[key] = null; return null;
    }
    _renderAvatarTo(cx, teamId, posId, state);
    this._cache[key] = can;
    return can;
  },

  clearCache() { this._cache = Object.create(null); },
};

/* ============================================================
   ANIMAL PALETTES — fur, accent, eye, nose colors per team.
   Each palette describes the species, not the team uniform; the
   uniform color comes from team.color and is layered on top.
   ============================================================ */
const ANIMAL_PALETTE = {
  fox: { fur: "#d97a3c", belly: "#f5e6d3", accent: "#a04a1f", nose: "#1a1a1a", eye: "#1a1a1a", inner: "#fce0c8" },
  bvr: { fur: "#8a5a3c", belly: "#c89a72", accent: "#5d3a22", nose: "#1a1a1a", eye: "#1a1a1a", inner: "#a87850", tooth: "#fefcf0" },
  owl: { fur: "#b89570", belly: "#d8c098", accent: "#7a5a3c", nose: "#1a1a1a", eye: "#1a1a1a", inner: "#5c4525", iris: "#f4c542" },
  shk: { fur: "#7a8a98", belly: "#dde4ea", accent: "#4a5560", nose: "#1a1a1a", eye: "#1a1a1a", inner: "#5c6770", tooth: "#fefcf0" },
  pnd: { fur: "#f0e8dc", belly: "#f8f4e8", accent: "#1a1a1a", nose: "#1a1a1a", eye: "#1a1a1a", inner: "#1a1a1a" },
  rcc: { fur: "#7e7468", belly: "#aaa094", accent: "#1a1a1a", nose: "#1a1a1a", eye: "#1a1a1a", inner: "#5c4f42", mask: "#1a1a1a" },
  frg: { fur: "#5fa84a", belly: "#a8d68f", accent: "#3a7c2c", nose: "#1a1a1a", eye: "#fefcf0", inner: "#3a7c2c", iris: "#1a1a1a" },
  hed: { fur: "#a08068", belly: "#d4b89e", accent: "#5a4530", nose: "#d878a8", eye: "#1a1a1a", inner: "#7a5e44", spike: "#5a4530" },
  kit: { fur: "#e8d5b8", belly: "#f5ebd6", accent: "#a88860", nose: "#d878a8", eye: "#1a1a1a", inner: "#fce0c8", whisker: "#5c4f42" },
  wlf: { fur: "#8a8c92", belly: "#c8c8cc", accent: "#3a3c40", nose: "#1a1a1a", eye: "#f4c542", inner: "#5c5e62", tooth: "#fefcf0" },
  bdg: { fur: "#1a1a1a", belly: "#fefcf0", accent: "#fefcf0", nose: "#1a1a1a", eye: "#1a1a1a", inner: "#fefcf0", stripe: "#fefcf0" },
  otr: { fur: "#7a5a3c", belly: "#c8a880", accent: "#4a3422", nose: "#1a1a1a", eye: "#1a1a1a", inner: "#a08060", whisker: "#3a2812" },
};

/* ============================================================
   COMPOSITION — assemble layers onto the offscreen canvas.
   ============================================================ */
function _renderAvatarTo(cx, teamId, posId, state) {
  const team = (typeof TEAM_BY_ID !== "undefined") ? TEAM_BY_ID[teamId] : null;
  const pal = ANIMAL_PALETTE[teamId] || ANIMAL_PALETTE.fox;
  const teamColor = (team && team.color) || "#888888";

  // Body geometry — torso roughly centered, head sits above
  const bx = AVATAR_W / 2;       // body center x
  const by = AVATAR_ANCHOR_Y;    // body center y (= player anchor)
  const hx = bx;                 // head center x
  const hy = by - 16;            // head center y

  cx.clearRect(0, 0, AVATAR_W, AVATAR_H);
  cx.lineJoin = "round";
  cx.lineCap = "round";

  // 1. Soft drop shadow under the body (very subtle; main shadow is
  //    drawn by drawMascot at the world level).
  cx.fillStyle = "rgba(0,0,0,0.18)";
  cx.beginPath();
  cx.ellipse(bx, by + 18, 14, 3, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  cx.fill();

  // 2. Cape (sniper only) — drawn behind the body so it hangs back
  if (posId === "sniper") {
    _drawCape(cx, bx, by, teamColor, pal);
  }

  // 3. Body silhouette + armor — varies by position
  _drawBodyArmor(cx, bx, by, teamColor, pal, posId);

  // 4. Head — animal-specific silhouette, ears, fur
  _drawAnimalHead(cx, hx, hy, teamId, pal);

  // 5. Face — eyes + mouth + accents, varies with state
  _drawAnimalFace(cx, hx, hy, teamId, pal, state);

  // 6. Helmet/hood — overlay drawn on top of head for tank/sniper/speed
  if (posId === "tank") {
    _drawTankHelmet(cx, hx, hy, teamColor);
  } else if (posId === "sniper") {
    _drawSniperHood(cx, hx, hy, teamColor, pal);
  } else if (posId === "speed") {
    _drawSpeedHood(cx, hx, hy, teamColor);
  }

  // 7. Position emblem on the chest (a tiny role glyph)
  _drawChestEmblem(cx, bx, by + 2, posId, teamColor);
}

/* ============================================================
   BODY + ARMOR per position. All bodies use a rounded torso
   silhouette with team-color armor; position-specific accents
   differentiate the seven classes.
   ============================================================ */
function _drawBodyArmor(cx, bx, by, teamColor, pal, posId) {
  const armor = teamColor;
  const armorDark = darken(teamColor, 0.30);
  const armorLite = lighten(teamColor, 0.25);

  // Position-driven width adjustments
  const isHeavy = posId === "tank" || posId === "brawl";
  const isLight = posId === "speed";
  const torsoW = isHeavy ? 18 : isLight ? 13 : 15;
  const torsoH = 14;

  // Main torso plate
  cx.fillStyle = armor;
  cx.beginPath();
  // rounded-rectangle torso
  const tx = bx - torsoW, ty = by - torsoH/2;
  const r = 4;
  cx.moveTo(tx + r, ty);
  cx.lineTo(tx + 2*torsoW - r, ty);
  cx.quadraticCurveTo(tx + 2*torsoW, ty, tx + 2*torsoW, ty + r);
  cx.lineTo(tx + 2*torsoW, ty + torsoH - r);
  cx.quadraticCurveTo(tx + 2*torsoW, ty + torsoH, tx + 2*torsoW - r, ty + torsoH);
  cx.lineTo(tx + r, ty + torsoH);
  cx.quadraticCurveTo(tx, ty + torsoH, tx, ty + torsoH - r);
  cx.lineTo(tx, ty + r);
  cx.quadraticCurveTo(tx, ty, tx + r, ty);
  cx.closePath();
  cx.fill();

  // Lower plate trim (darker accent strip)
  cx.fillStyle = armorDark;
  cx.fillRect(tx, ty + torsoH - 3, torsoW * 2, 3);

  // Position-specific overlays
  if (posId === "tank") {
    // Heavy shoulder pads — rounded cubes either side
    cx.fillStyle = armorDark;
    cx.beginPath();
    cx.arc(bx - torsoW + 1, ty + 2, 4, 0, Math.PI * 2);
    cx.arc(bx + torsoW - 1, ty + 2, 4, 0, Math.PI * 2);
    cx.fill();
    // Center plate band
    cx.fillStyle = armorLite;
    cx.fillRect(bx - 6, ty + 2, 12, 2);
  } else if (posId === "striker") {
    // Sharp pauldron spikes
    cx.fillStyle = armorDark;
    cx.beginPath();
    cx.moveTo(bx - torsoW, ty + 1);
    cx.lineTo(bx - torsoW - 3, ty + 6);
    cx.lineTo(bx - torsoW + 1, ty + 5);
    cx.closePath();
    cx.moveTo(bx + torsoW, ty + 1);
    cx.lineTo(bx + torsoW + 3, ty + 6);
    cx.lineTo(bx + torsoW - 1, ty + 5);
    cx.closePath();
    cx.fill();
  } else if (posId === "speed") {
    // Slim shoulder seams + chevron
    cx.strokeStyle = armorLite;
    cx.lineWidth = 1;
    cx.beginPath();
    cx.moveTo(bx - 5, ty + 8);
    cx.lineTo(bx, ty + 5);
    cx.lineTo(bx + 5, ty + 8);
    cx.stroke();
  } else if (posId === "brawl") {
    // Heavy bracers/gauntlets at sides (flanking the torso)
    cx.fillStyle = armorDark;
    cx.fillRect(tx - 3, ty + 2, 3, 10);
    cx.fillRect(tx + 2*torsoW, ty + 2, 3, 10);
    cx.fillStyle = armorLite;
    cx.fillRect(tx - 3, ty + 4, 3, 1);
    cx.fillRect(tx + 2*torsoW, ty + 4, 3, 1);
  } else if (posId === "healer") {
    // White cross on the chest — universal medic emblem
    cx.fillStyle = "#fefcf0";
    cx.fillRect(bx - 1, ty + 3, 2, 8);
    cx.fillRect(bx - 4, ty + 6, 8, 2);
  } else if (posId === "grenadier") {
    // Bandolier diagonal across chest with grenade beads
    cx.strokeStyle = armorDark;
    cx.lineWidth = 2.5;
    cx.beginPath();
    cx.moveTo(bx - torsoW + 2, ty + 1);
    cx.lineTo(bx + torsoW - 2, ty + torsoH - 4);
    cx.stroke();
    // Three small grenade beads
    cx.fillStyle = "#3a3c40";
    for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
      const t = (i + 1) / 4;
      cx.beginPath();
      cx.arc(bx - torsoW + 2 + (2*torsoW - 4) * t, ty + 1 + (torsoH - 5) * t, 1.4, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      cx.fill();
    }
  }
  // sniper has its own cape drawn earlier; no extra body overlay needed
}

/* ============================================================
   CAPE — drawn behind the body for snipers.
   ============================================================ */
function _drawCape(cx, bx, by, teamColor, pal) {
  const cape = darken(teamColor, 0.45);
  const trim = darken(teamColor, 0.6);
  cx.fillStyle = cape;
  cx.beginPath();
  cx.moveTo(bx - 14, by - 8);
  cx.lineTo(bx - 18, by + 14);
  cx.lineTo(bx, by + 18);
  cx.lineTo(bx + 18, by + 14);
  cx.lineTo(bx + 14, by - 8);
  cx.closePath();
  cx.fill();
  // Cape inner shadow center fold
  cx.fillStyle = trim;
  cx.beginPath();
  cx.moveTo(bx, by - 6);
  cx.lineTo(bx - 1, by + 16);
  cx.lineTo(bx + 1, by + 16);
  cx.closePath();
  cx.fill();
}

/* ============================================================
   ANIMAL HEAD — silhouette + ears + species markings.
   Dispatches by teamId; each species draws its distinctive shape.
   ============================================================ */
function _drawAnimalHead(cx, hx, hy, teamId, pal) {
  switch (teamId) {
    case "fox": _headFox(cx, hx, hy, pal); break;
    case "bvr": _headBeaver(cx, hx, hy, pal); break;
    case "owl": _headOwl(cx, hx, hy, pal); break;
    case "shk": _headShark(cx, hx, hy, pal); break;
    case "pnd": _headPanda(cx, hx, hy, pal); break;
    case "rcc": _headRaccoon(cx, hx, hy, pal); break;
    case "frg": _headFrog(cx, hx, hy, pal); break;
    case "hed": _headHedgehog(cx, hx, hy, pal); break;
    case "kit": _headKit(cx, hx, hy, pal); break;
    case "wlf": _headWolf(cx, hx, hy, pal); break;
    case "bdg": _headBadger(cx, hx, hy, pal); break;
    case "otr": _headOtter(cx, hx, hy, pal); break;
    default:    _headFox(cx, hx, hy, pal); break;
  }
}

// Fox — pointed ears, narrow tapered snout, white chin
function _headFox(cx, hx, hy, pal) {
  // Ears — triangular, tipped darker, pink interior
  cx.fillStyle = pal.accent;
  cx.beginPath();
  cx.moveTo(hx - 8, hy - 4);
  cx.lineTo(hx - 11, hy - 12);
  cx.lineTo(hx - 5, hy - 8);
  cx.closePath();
  cx.moveTo(hx + 8, hy - 4);
  cx.lineTo(hx + 11, hy - 12);
  cx.lineTo(hx + 5, hy - 8);
  cx.closePath();
  cx.fill();
  cx.fillStyle = pal.inner;
  cx.beginPath();
  cx.moveTo(hx - 8, hy - 5);
  cx.lineTo(hx - 9, hy - 9);
  cx.lineTo(hx - 6, hy - 8);
  cx.closePath();
  cx.moveTo(hx + 8, hy - 5);
  cx.lineTo(hx + 9, hy - 9);
  cx.lineTo(hx + 6, hy - 8);
  cx.closePath();
  cx.fill();
  // Head — oval, slightly tapered to chin
  cx.fillStyle = pal.fur;
  cx.beginPath();
  cx.ellipse(hx, hy, 9, 8.5, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  cx.fill();
  // White snout/chin patch
  cx.fillStyle = pal.belly;
  cx.beginPath();
  cx.ellipse(hx, hy + 3, 5, 4, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  cx.fill();
}

// Beaver — round head, small rounded ears, big teeth
function _headBeaver(cx, hx, hy, pal) {
  // Round small ears
  cx.fillStyle = pal.accent;
  cx.beginPath();
  cx.arc(hx - 8, hy - 7, 2.5, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  cx.arc(hx + 8, hy - 7, 2.5, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  cx.fill();
  cx.fillStyle = pal.inner;
  cx.beginPath();
  cx.arc(hx - 8, hy - 7, 1.2, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  cx.arc(hx + 8, hy - 7, 1.2, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  cx.fill();
  // Round head
  cx.fillStyle = pal.fur;
  cx.beginPath();
  cx.arc(hx, hy, 9, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  cx.fill();
  // Lighter muzzle area
  cx.fillStyle = pal.belly;
  cx.beginPath();
  cx.ellipse(hx, hy + 3, 5, 3.5, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  cx.fill();
}

// Owl — very round, big yellow-ringed eyes, small ear tufts, beak
function _headOwl(cx, hx, hy, pal) {
  // Tiny ear tufts (peaked)
  cx.fillStyle = pal.accent;
  cx.beginPath();
  cx.moveTo(hx - 7, hy - 6);
  cx.lineTo(hx - 8, hy - 11);
  cx.lineTo(hx - 5, hy - 7);
  cx.closePath();
  cx.moveTo(hx + 7, hy - 6);
  cx.lineTo(hx + 8, hy - 11);
  cx.lineTo(hx + 5, hy - 7);
  cx.closePath();
  cx.fill();
  // Round head
  cx.fillStyle = pal.fur;
  cx.beginPath();
  cx.arc(hx, hy, 10, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  cx.fill();
  // Feather mottling — three darker dots
  cx.fillStyle = pal.accent;
  cx.beginPath();
  cx.arc(hx - 4, hy + 5, 1, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  cx.arc(hx + 4, hy + 5, 1, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  cx.arc(hx, hy + 7, 1, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  cx.fill();
}

// Shark — pointed snout, dorsal fin, white belly underside
function _headShark(cx, hx, hy, pal) {
  // Dorsal fin on top of head
  cx.fillStyle = pal.accent;
  cx.beginPath();
  cx.moveTo(hx - 2, hy - 9);
  cx.lineTo(hx, hy - 14);
  cx.lineTo(hx + 4, hy - 8);
  cx.closePath();
  cx.fill();
  // Head — pointed-snout oval
  cx.fillStyle = pal.fur;
  cx.beginPath();
  cx.ellipse(hx, hy, 10, 7.5, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  cx.fill();
  // White belly underside
  cx.fillStyle = pal.belly;
  cx.beginPath();
  cx.ellipse(hx, hy + 4, 9, 3, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  cx.fill();
  // Pointed snout tip
  cx.fillStyle = pal.fur;
  cx.beginPath();
  cx.moveTo(hx + 8, hy + 1);
  cx.lineTo(hx + 12, hy + 3);
  cx.lineTo(hx + 8, hy + 4);
  cx.closePath();
  cx.fill();
}

// Panda — white face, black eye patches, black ears
function _headPanda(cx, hx, hy, pal) {
  // Black ears
  cx.fillStyle = pal.accent;
  cx.beginPath();
  cx.arc(hx - 8, hy - 8, 3, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  cx.arc(hx + 8, hy - 8, 3, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  cx.fill();
  // Round white head
  cx.fillStyle = pal.fur;
  cx.beginPath();
  cx.arc(hx, hy, 9.5, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  cx.fill();
  // Black eye patches (oval, tilted slightly)
  cx.fillStyle = pal.accent;
  cx.beginPath();
  cx.ellipse(hx - 4, hy - 1, 3, 4, -0.3, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  cx.ellipse(hx + 4, hy - 1, 3, 4, 0.3, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  cx.fill();
}

// Raccoon — black mask stripe across eyes, pointed ears
function _headRaccoon(cx, hx, hy, pal) {
  // Pointed ears with black tips
  cx.fillStyle = pal.fur;
  cx.beginPath();
  cx.moveTo(hx - 8, hy - 5);
  cx.lineTo(hx - 10, hy - 11);
  cx.lineTo(hx - 4, hy - 8);
  cx.closePath();
  cx.moveTo(hx + 8, hy - 5);
  cx.lineTo(hx + 10, hy - 11);
  cx.lineTo(hx + 4, hy - 8);
  cx.closePath();
  cx.fill();
  cx.fillStyle = pal.accent;
  cx.beginPath();
  cx.arc(hx - 9, hy - 10, 1.4, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  cx.arc(hx + 9, hy - 10, 1.4, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  cx.fill();
  // Head — slightly triangular
  cx.fillStyle = pal.fur;
  cx.beginPath();
  cx.ellipse(hx, hy + 1, 9, 8, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  cx.fill();
  // Black mask stripe across eyes
  cx.fillStyle = pal.mask;
  cx.beginPath();
  cx.ellipse(hx, hy - 1, 8, 2.5, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  cx.fill();
  // Light snout
  cx.fillStyle = pal.belly;
  cx.beginPath();
  cx.ellipse(hx, hy + 4, 4, 2.5, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  cx.fill();
}

// Frog — bulging eyes ON TOP of head, wide mouth, smooth green
function _headFrog(cx, hx, hy, pal) {
  // Two big bulging eye DOMES on top of head — drawn before head so
  // the head silhouette overlaps slightly at the base.
  cx.fillStyle = pal.fur;
  cx.beginPath();
  cx.arc(hx - 5, hy - 7, 4.5, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  cx.arc(hx + 5, hy - 7, 4.5, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  cx.fill();
  // Head — wide blob, low forehead
  cx.fillStyle = pal.fur;
  cx.beginPath();
  cx.ellipse(hx, hy + 2, 10, 7, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  cx.fill();
  // Lighter belly/chin underside
  cx.fillStyle = pal.belly;
  cx.beginPath();
  cx.ellipse(hx, hy + 5, 8, 3, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  cx.fill();
}

// Hedgehog — short spikes radiating from top, pointed snout, pink nose
function _headHedgehog(cx, hx, hy, pal) {
  // Spike crown — multiple short triangles around upper hemisphere
  cx.fillStyle = pal.spike;
  for (let i = 0; i < 9; i++) {
    const ang = -Math.PI + (i / 8) * Math.PI; // top hemisphere
    const r1 = 8.5, r2 = 12;
    const sx = hx + Math.cos(ang) * r1;
    const sy = hy + Math.sin(ang) * r1;
    const tx = hx + Math.cos(ang) * r2;
    const ty = hy + Math.sin(ang) * r2;
    cx.beginPath();
    cx.moveTo(sx - 1.5, sy + 1);
    cx.lineTo(tx, ty);
    cx.lineTo(sx + 1.5, sy + 1);
    cx.closePath();
    cx.fill();
  }
  // Round head with pointed snout
  cx.fillStyle = pal.fur;
  cx.beginPath();
  cx.ellipse(hx, hy + 1, 8.5, 7.5, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  cx.fill();
  // Light snout
  cx.fillStyle = pal.belly;
  cx.beginPath();
  cx.ellipse(hx, hy + 4, 4.5, 3, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  cx.fill();
}

// Kit — triangular ears, pink nose, whiskers
function _headKit(cx, hx, hy, pal) {
  // Triangular ears with pink interior
  cx.fillStyle = pal.accent;
  cx.beginPath();
  cx.moveTo(hx - 7, hy - 6);
  cx.lineTo(hx - 9, hy - 13);
  cx.lineTo(hx - 3, hy - 8);
  cx.closePath();
  cx.moveTo(hx + 7, hy - 6);
  cx.lineTo(hx + 9, hy - 13);
  cx.lineTo(hx + 3, hy - 8);
  cx.closePath();
  cx.fill();
  cx.fillStyle = pal.nose;
  cx.beginPath();
  cx.moveTo(hx - 7, hy - 7);
  cx.lineTo(hx - 8, hy - 11);
  cx.lineTo(hx - 5, hy - 8);
  cx.closePath();
  cx.moveTo(hx + 7, hy - 7);
  cx.lineTo(hx + 8, hy - 11);
  cx.lineTo(hx + 5, hy - 8);
  cx.closePath();
  cx.fill();
  // Round head
  cx.fillStyle = pal.fur;
  cx.beginPath();
  cx.arc(hx, hy, 9, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  cx.fill();
  // Whiskers (3 lines per side)
  cx.strokeStyle = pal.whisker;
  cx.lineWidth = 0.6;
  for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
    const yo = hy + 2 + i * 1.5;
    cx.beginPath();
    cx.moveTo(hx - 4, yo);
    cx.lineTo(hx - 10, yo + (i - 1) * 0.8);
    cx.moveTo(hx + 4, yo);
    cx.lineTo(hx + 10, yo + (i - 1) * 0.8);
    cx.stroke();
  }
}

// Wolf — pointed ears, sharp snout, yellow eyes
function _headWolf(cx, hx, hy, pal) {
  // Pointed ears
  cx.fillStyle = pal.accent;
  cx.beginPath();
  cx.moveTo(hx - 7, hy - 5);
  cx.lineTo(hx - 10, hy - 13);
  cx.lineTo(hx - 4, hy - 9);
  cx.closePath();
  cx.moveTo(hx + 7, hy - 5);
  cx.lineTo(hx + 10, hy - 13);
  cx.lineTo(hx + 4, hy - 9);
  cx.closePath();
  cx.fill();
  cx.fillStyle = pal.inner;
  cx.beginPath();
  cx.moveTo(hx - 7, hy - 6);
  cx.lineTo(hx - 9, hy - 11);
  cx.lineTo(hx - 5, hy - 8);
  cx.closePath();
  cx.moveTo(hx + 7, hy - 6);
  cx.lineTo(hx + 9, hy - 11);
  cx.lineTo(hx + 5, hy - 8);
  cx.closePath();
  cx.fill();
  // Head — somewhat angular
  cx.fillStyle = pal.fur;
  cx.beginPath();
  cx.ellipse(hx, hy, 9, 8, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  cx.fill();
  // Light underside
  cx.fillStyle = pal.belly;
  cx.beginPath();
  cx.ellipse(hx, hy + 4, 6, 3, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  cx.fill();
  // Sharp snout extension
  cx.fillStyle = pal.fur;
  cx.beginPath();
  cx.moveTo(hx - 3, hy + 4);
  cx.lineTo(hx, hy + 9);
  cx.lineTo(hx + 3, hy + 4);
  cx.closePath();
  cx.fill();
}

// Badger — black with white stripe down center of face
function _headBadger(cx, hx, hy, pal) {
  // Small rounded ears
  cx.fillStyle = pal.fur;
  cx.beginPath();
  cx.arc(hx - 8, hy - 7, 2.2, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  cx.arc(hx + 8, hy - 7, 2.2, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  cx.fill();
  // Wide low head
  cx.fillStyle = pal.fur;
  cx.beginPath();
  cx.ellipse(hx, hy + 1, 9.5, 8, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  cx.fill();
  // White stripe down center of face (forehead to nose)
  cx.fillStyle = pal.stripe;
  cx.beginPath();
  cx.moveTo(hx - 1.5, hy - 7);
  cx.lineTo(hx + 1.5, hy - 7);
  cx.lineTo(hx + 2, hy + 6);
  cx.lineTo(hx - 2, hy + 6);
  cx.closePath();
  cx.fill();
}

// Otter — rounded head, light belly, whiskers
function _headOtter(cx, hx, hy, pal) {
  // Tiny ears
  cx.fillStyle = pal.accent;
  cx.beginPath();
  cx.arc(hx - 7, hy - 7, 1.8, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  cx.arc(hx + 7, hy - 7, 1.8, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  cx.fill();
  // Rounded head
  cx.fillStyle = pal.fur;
  cx.beginPath();
  cx.ellipse(hx, hy, 9, 8, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  cx.fill();
  // Light belly/chin
  cx.fillStyle = pal.belly;
  cx.beginPath();
  cx.ellipse(hx, hy + 4, 6, 3, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  cx.fill();
  // Whiskers
  cx.strokeStyle = pal.whisker;
  cx.lineWidth = 0.5;
  for (let i = 0; i < 2; i++) {
    cx.beginPath();
    cx.moveTo(hx - 3, hy + 3 + i);
    cx.lineTo(hx - 8, hy + 3 + i + (i - 0.5) * 0.5);
    cx.moveTo(hx + 3, hy + 3 + i);
    cx.lineTo(hx + 8, hy + 3 + i + (i - 0.5) * 0.5);
    cx.stroke();
  }
}

/* ============================================================
   FACE — eyes + mouth + nose, varies with state.
   Same eye/mouth treatment for all species since they're already
   distinguished by head silhouette; this saves 12*3 = 36 face
   variations and keeps the state distinction crisp.
   ============================================================ */
function _drawAnimalFace(cx, hx, hy, teamId, pal, state) {
  // Eye positions — owl/frog get wider-set eyes; everyone else uses default
  let eyeOffsetX = 3;
  let eyeOffsetY = -1;
  let eyeR = 1.4;
  let useIris = false;
  if (teamId === "owl") {
    eyeOffsetX = 3.5; eyeOffsetY = -1; eyeR = 2.4; useIris = true;
  } else if (teamId === "frg") {
    // Eyes on top of head (drawn within the bulging domes)
    eyeOffsetX = 5; eyeOffsetY = -7; eyeR = 1.8;
  } else if (teamId === "shk") {
    eyeOffsetX = 4; eyeOffsetY = -2; eyeR = 1;
  } else if (teamId === "wlf") {
    useIris = true;
  } else if (teamId === "bdg") {
    // Badger eyes flank the white stripe
    eyeOffsetX = 4; eyeOffsetY = -1;
  }

  if (state === "stunned") {
    // X-shaped closed eyes — universal "knocked silly"
    cx.strokeStyle = "#1a1a1a";
    cx.lineWidth = 1.3;
    for (const sign of [-1, 1]) {
      const ex = hx + eyeOffsetX * sign;
      const ey = hy + eyeOffsetY;
      cx.beginPath();
      cx.moveTo(ex - 1.5, ey - 1.5);
      cx.lineTo(ex + 1.5, ey + 1.5);
      cx.moveTo(ex + 1.5, ey - 1.5);
      cx.lineTo(ex - 1.5, ey + 1.5);
      cx.stroke();
    }
  } else if (state === "critical") {
    // Wide intense eyes — bigger pupils, slight squint
    cx.fillStyle = "#fefcf0";
    cx.beginPath();
    cx.arc(hx - eyeOffsetX, hy + eyeOffsetY, eyeR + 0.6, 0, Math.PI * 2);
    cx.arc(hx + eyeOffsetX, hy + eyeOffsetY, eyeR + 0.6, 0, Math.PI * 2);
    cx.fill();
    cx.fillStyle = "#1a1a1a";
    cx.beginPath();
    cx.arc(hx - eyeOffsetX, hy + eyeOffsetY, eyeR + 0.2, 0, Math.PI * 2);
    cx.arc(hx + eyeOffsetX, hy + eyeOffsetY, eyeR + 0.2, 0, Math.PI * 2);
    cx.fill();
  } else {
    // Default alive — small focused eyes
    if (useIris) {
      // Owl/wolf: yellow ring + black pupil
      cx.fillStyle = pal.iris || "#f4c542";
      cx.beginPath();
      cx.arc(hx - eyeOffsetX, hy + eyeOffsetY, eyeR + 0.7, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      cx.arc(hx + eyeOffsetX, hy + eyeOffsetY, eyeR + 0.7, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      cx.fill();
      cx.fillStyle = "#1a1a1a";
      cx.beginPath();
      cx.arc(hx - eyeOffsetX, hy + eyeOffsetY, eyeR * 0.65, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      cx.arc(hx + eyeOffsetX, hy + eyeOffsetY, eyeR * 0.65, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      cx.fill();
    } else if (teamId === "frg") {
      // Frog: white sclera in dome, black pupil
      cx.fillStyle = "#fefcf0";
      cx.beginPath();
      cx.arc(hx - eyeOffsetX, hy + eyeOffsetY, eyeR + 1, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      cx.arc(hx + eyeOffsetX, hy + eyeOffsetY, eyeR + 1, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      cx.fill();
      cx.fillStyle = "#1a1a1a";
      cx.beginPath();
      cx.arc(hx - eyeOffsetX, hy + eyeOffsetY, eyeR, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      cx.arc(hx + eyeOffsetX, hy + eyeOffsetY, eyeR, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      cx.fill();
    } else {
      cx.fillStyle = pal.eye;
      cx.beginPath();
      cx.arc(hx - eyeOffsetX, hy + eyeOffsetY, eyeR, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      cx.arc(hx + eyeOffsetX, hy + eyeOffsetY, eyeR, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      cx.fill();
    }
  }

  // Mouth/nose — varies by team. Keep simple for readability.
  if (teamId === "bvr") {
    // Beaver: two big visible front teeth
    cx.fillStyle = pal.tooth;
    cx.fillRect(hx - 1.6, hy + 4, 1.4, 3);
    cx.fillRect(hx + 0.2, hy + 4, 1.4, 3);
    cx.strokeStyle = pal.accent;
    cx.lineWidth = 0.4;
    cx.strokeRect(hx - 1.6, hy + 4, 1.4, 3);
    cx.strokeRect(hx + 0.2, hy + 4, 1.4, 3);
  } else if (teamId === "shk") {
    // Shark: visible teeth row
